// =============================================================
// src/components/documents/DocumentTree.tsx
// Левое дерево документов (иерархия папок Data Room).
// Для юриста даёт:
// - быстрое понимание структуры дата-рума;
// - статус обработки документа;
// - уровень классификации (L0–L4 / manual) в компактном виде.
// =============================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import type { Document, ClassificationLevel } from "../../types";
import {
  ChevronRight,
  ChevronDown,
  Folder,
  FileText,
  Search,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "../../utils";
import { ClassificationBadge } from "../ui/ClassificationBadge";
import { DOCUMENT_STATUS_CONFIG } from "../../utils/statusConfig";

/* ---------- Публичные пропсы ---------- */

interface Props {
  documents: Document[];
  selectedDocId: string | null;
  onSelectDoc: (doc: Document) => void;
  /** Показывать ли компактный бейдж классификации. */
  showClassification?: boolean;
  /** Можно ли удалять документы (admin/editor). */
  canDelete?: boolean;
  /** Колбэк для удаления документа. */
  onDeleteDoc?: (doc: Document) => void;
}

/* ---------- Внутренние типы ---------- */

/** Узел дерева: папка с дочерними папками и файлами. */
interface TreeNode {
  name: string; // Имя папки
  fullPath: string; // Полный путь от корня
  children: TreeNode[]; // Дочерние папки
  docs: Document[]; // Файлы в этой папке
}

/* ====================================================================
   Главный компонент
   ==================================================================== */

export const DocumentTree: React.FC<Props> = ({
  documents,
  selectedDocId,
  onSelectDoc,
  showClassification = true,
  canDelete = false,
  onDeleteDoc,
}) => {
  const [searchText, setSearchText] = useState("");
  // По умолчанию раскрыт корень
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set([""]));

  /* --- Построение дерева --- */
  const rootNode = useMemo(() => buildTree(documents), [documents]);

  /* --- Фильтрация по поиску --- */
  const displayTree = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchText.trim()) return rootNode;
    return filterTree(rootNode, searchText.toLowerCase());
  }, [rootNode, searchText]);

  /* --- Клик по папке: развернуть / свернуть --- */
  const toggle = (path: string) => {
    setExpanded((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(path)) next.delete(path);
      else next.add(path);
      return next;
    });
  };

  /* --- Подсчёт документов --- */
  const totalShown = countDocs(displayTree);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white">
      {/* ---------- Поиск ---------- */}
      <div className="p-3 border-b">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"
            size={14}
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Поиск по имени документа…"
            value={searchText}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchText(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-sm border rounded focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
          />
        </div>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1.5">
          Показано: {totalShown} из {documents.length} документов
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- Дерево ---------- */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1 py-2">
        {totalShown === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-center text-gray-400 text-sm py-10">
            Документы не найдены
          </p>
        ) : (
          <NodeView
            node={displayTree}
            expanded={expanded}
            onToggle={toggle}
            selectedDocId={selectedDocId}
            onSelectDoc={onSelectDoc}
            depth={0}
            showClassification={showClassification}
            canDelete={canDelete}
            onDeleteDoc={onDeleteDoc}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ====================================================================
   Рекурсивный компонент «узел дерева»
   ==================================================================== */

interface NodeViewProps {
  node: TreeNode;
  expanded: Set<string>;
  onToggle: (path: string) => void;
  selectedDocId: string | null;
  onSelectDoc: (doc: Document) => void;
  depth: number;
  showClassification: boolean;
  canDelete: boolean;
  onDeleteDoc?: (doc: Document) => void;
}

const NodeView: React.FC<NodeViewProps> = ({
  node,
  expanded,
  onToggle,
  selectedDocId,
  onSelectDoc,
  depth,
  showClassification,
  canDelete,
  onDeleteDoc,
}) => {
  const isOpen = expanded.has(node.fullPath);
  const hasContent = node.children.length > 0 || node.docs.length > 0;
  const paddingLeft = depth * 14 + 8;

  return (
    <>
      {/* ---- Строка папки (пропускаем для корня) ---- */}
      {node.name && (
        <div
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2 rounded hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer select-none"
          style={{ paddingLeft }}
          onClick={() => hasContent && onToggle(node.fullPath)}
        >
          {/* Стрелка */}
          {hasContent ? (
            isOpen ? (
              <ChevronDown size={14} className="text-gray-400 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-gray-400 shrink-0" />
            )
          ) : (
            <span className="w-3.5 shrink-0" />
          )}

          {/* Иконка папки */}
          <Folder size={14} className="text-blue-500 shrink-0" />

          {/* Имя */}
          <span className="flex-1 text-sm text-gray-700 truncate">
            {node.name}
          </span>

          {/* Счётчик документов в поддереве */}
          <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 shrink-0">
            {countDocs(node)}
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ---- Содержимое (дочерние папки + файлы) ---- */}
      {isOpen && (
        <>
          {/* Дочерние папки */}
          {node.children.map((child) => (
            <NodeView
              key={child.fullPath}
              node={child}
              expanded={expanded}
              onToggle={onToggle}
              selectedDocId={selectedDocId}
              onSelectDoc={onSelectDoc}
              depth={depth + 1}
              showClassification={showClassification}
              canDelete={canDelete}
              onDeleteDoc={onDeleteDoc}
            />
          ))}

          {/* Файлы в текущей папке */}
          {node.docs.map((doc) => {
            const config = DOCUMENT_STATUS_CONFIG[doc.status];
            const needsReview = doc.classification_requires_review;

            return (
              <div
                key={doc.id}
                className={cn(
                  "flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2 rounded cursor-pointer text-sm",
                  selectedDocId === doc.id
                    ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 ring-1 ring-blue-300"
                    : "hover:bg-gray-50 text-gray-700",
                )}
                style={{ paddingLeft: (depth + 1) * 14 + 8 }}
                onClick={() => onSelectDoc(doc)}
              >
                <FileText size={13} className="text-gray-400 shrink-0" />

                <span className="flex-1 truncate">{doc.filename}</span>

                {/* Бейдж классификации (компактный) */}
                {showClassification && doc.classification_level && (
                  <ClassificationBadge
                    level={doc.classification_level as ClassificationLevel}
                    confidence={doc.classification_confidence}
                    showConfidence={false}
                    className="shrink-0 ml-1"
                  />
                )}

                {/* Бейдж "нужна проверка" при low-confidence/unknown */}
                {needsReview && (
                  <span
                    className="ml-1 shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-yellow-50 text-[11px] text-yellow-700 border border-yellow-200"
                    title="Классификатор не уверен, требуется ручной просмотр"
                  >
                    нужна проверка
                  </span>
                )}

                {/* Явный статус документа */}
                <span
                  className={cn(
                    "shrink-0 ml-2 text-[11px] uppercase",
                    config?.color === "success"
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : config?.color === "warning"
                      ? "text-yellow-700"
                      : config?.color === "error"
                      ? "text-red-600"
                      : "text-gray-500",
                  )}
                  title={config?.label || doc.status}
                >
                  {config?.label || doc.status}
                </span>

                {/* Кнопка удаления (если разрешено) */}
                {canDelete && onDeleteDoc && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      onDeleteDoc(doc);
                    }}
                    className="ml-1 text-[11px] text-red-500 hover:text-red-700"
                    title="Удалить документ"
                  >
                    ✕
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
};

/* ====================================================================
   Утилиты: построение дерева, фильтрация, подсчёт
   ==================================================================== */

/** Построить дерево из плоского списка документов. */
function buildTree(documents: Document[]): TreeNode {
  const root: TreeNode = { name: "", fullPath: "", children: [], docs: [] };

  for (const doc of documents) {
    const parts = (doc.folder_path || "")
      .split("/")
      .filter((p) => p.length > 0);

    let current = root;

    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
      const part = parts[i];
      const path = parts.slice(0, i + 1).join("/");
      let child = current.children.find((c) => c.name === part);
      if (!child) {
        child = { name: part, fullPath: path, children: [], docs: [] };
        current.children.push(child);
      }
      current = child;
    }

    current.docs.push(doc);
  }

  sortTree(root);
  return root;
}

/** Рекурсивная сортировка: папки по имени, файлы по имени. */
function sortTree(node: TreeNode) {
  node.children.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ru"));
  node.docs.sort((a, b) => a.filename.localeCompare(b.filename, "ru"));
  node.children.forEach(sortTree);
}

/** Фильтрация дерева: оставить только совпадающие файлы и их родительские папки. */
function filterTree(node: TreeNode, searchLower: string): TreeNode {
  const matchingDocs = node.docs.filter((d) =>
    d.filename.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower),
  );

  const matchingChildren = node.children
    .map((c) => filterTree(c, searchLower))
    .filter((c) => c.children.length > 0 || c.docs.length > 0);

  return { ...node, docs: matchingDocs, children: matchingChildren };
}

/** Рекурсивный подсчёт документов во всём поддереве. */
function countDocs(node: TreeNode): number {
  return (
    node.docs.length + node.children.reduce((sum, c) => sum + countDocs(c), 0)
  );
}

export default DocumentTree;

/**
 * LayoutDebugModal
 *
 * Визуальное сравнение документа до и после PII-sanitation.
 *
 * Что делает:
 * - показывает две синхронно прокручиваемые колонки;
 * - слева отображает raw layout (исходный текст);
 * - справа отображает sanitized layout (текст после санитизации);
 * - сопоставляет строки сначала по elementid, затем эвристически по похожести текста внутри страницы;
 * - в левой колонке подсвечивает только изменившиеся слова внутри строки;
 * - если строка удалена полностью, подсвечивает весь исходный текст как удалённый.
 *
 * Для чего нужен:
 * - быстро проверить, как именно изменился документ после санитизации;
 * - понять, какие слова были замаскированы, заменены или удалены;
 * - визуально валидировать результат PII pipeline без чтения JSON.
 *
 * Важно:
 * - backend debug endpoint возвращает raw/sanitized JSON только если включены
 *   query params includeraw=true и includesanitized=true;
 * - этот компонент предполагает, что useDocumentLayoutsDebug делает именно такой запрос.
 */

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { X, Loader2, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { useDocumentLayoutsDebug } from "../../api/documents";

interface Props {
  docId: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

type LayoutElement = {
  elementid?: string;
  text?: string;
  page?: number;
  bbox?: number[];
};

type LayoutPayload = {
  elements?: LayoutElement[];
};

type LayoutsDebugResponse = {
  documentid?: string;
  dealid?: string;
  rawlayoutref?: string | null;
  sanitizedlayoutref?: string | null;
  rawlayout?: LayoutPayload | null;
  sanitizedlayout?: LayoutPayload | null;

  // fallback for camelCase if frontend mapper normalizes keys
  documentId?: string;
  dealId?: string;
  rawLayoutRef?: string | null;
  sanitizedLayoutRef?: string | null;
  rawLayout?: LayoutPayload | null;
  sanitizedLayout?: LayoutPayload | null;
};

type CompareRow = {
  key: string;
  page: number;
  beforeText: string;
  afterText: string;
  changed: boolean;
  removed: boolean;
  added: boolean;
  matchType: "id" | "heuristic" | "removed" | "added";
};

type DiffToken =
  | { type: "same"; text: string }
  | { type: "changed"; text: string }
  | { type: "space"; text: string };

function isLayoutPayload(value: unknown): value is LayoutPayload {
  return !!value && typeof value === "object";
}

function normalizeText(text?: string): string {
  return (text || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

function tokenizeForSimilarity(text: string): string[] {
  return normalizeText(text)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu, " ")
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function tokenizeWithSpaces(text: string): string[] {
  if (!text) return [];
  return text.match(/\S+|\s+/g) || [];
}

function isWhitespace(token: string): boolean {
  return /^\s+$/.test(token);
}

function normalizeWordForDiff(word: string): string {
  return word.toLowerCase().replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]/gu, "").trim();
}

function textSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {
  const aa = tokenizeForSimilarity(a);
  const bb = tokenizeForSimilarity(b);

  if (!aa.length && !bb.length) return 1;
  if (!aa.length || !bb.length) return 0;

  const setA = new Set(aa);
  const setB = new Set(bb);

  let intersection = 0;
  for (const token of setA) {
    if (setB.has(token)) intersection += 1;
  }

  const union = new Set([...setA, ...setB]).size;
  const jaccard = union === 0 ? 0 : intersection / union;

  const lenA = normalizeText(a).length;
  const lenB = normalizeText(b).length;
  const maxLen = Math.max(lenA, lenB, 1);
  const lenPenalty = 1 - Math.abs(lenA - lenB) / maxLen;

  return jaccard * 0.8 + Math.max(0, lenPenalty) * 0.2;
}

function sortElements(elements: LayoutElement[]): LayoutElement[] {
  return [...elements].sort((a, b) => {
    const ap = typeof a.page === "number" ? a.page : 0;
    const bp = typeof b.page === "number" ? b.page : 0;
    if (ap !== bp) return ap - bp;

    const ay = Array.isArray(a.bbox) ? (a.bbox[1] ?? 0) : 0;
    const by = Array.isArray(b.bbox) ? (b.bbox[1] ?? 0) : 0;
    if (Math.abs(ay - by) > 3) return ay - by;

    const ax = Array.isArray(a.bbox) ? (a.bbox[0] ?? 0) : 0;
    const bx = Array.isArray(b.bbox) ? (b.bbox[0] ?? 0) : 0;
    return ax - bx;
  });
}

function getElements(layout: unknown): LayoutElement[] {
  if (!isLayoutPayload(layout) || !Array.isArray(layout.elements)) return [];
  return sortElements(layout.elements);
}

function extractRawLayout(data: LayoutsDebugResponse | undefined): unknown {
  return data?.rawlayout ?? data?.rawLayout ?? null;
}

function extractSanitizedLayout(data: LayoutsDebugResponse | undefined): unknown {
  return data?.sanitizedlayout ?? data?.sanitizedLayout ?? null;
}

function buildCompareRows(rawLayout: unknown, sanitizedLayout: unknown): CompareRow[] {
  const rawElements = getElements(rawLayout);
  const sanitizedElements = getElements(sanitizedLayout);

  const rows: CompareRow[] = [];
  const usedSanitizedIndexes = new Set<number>();

  const sanitizedById = new Map<string, { el: LayoutElement; index: number }>();
  sanitizedElements.forEach((el, index) => {
    if (el.elementid) sanitizedById.set(el.elementid, { el, index });
  });

  const rawByPage = new Map<number, Array<{ el: LayoutElement; index: number }>>();
  const sanitizedByPage = new Map<number, Array<{ el: LayoutElement; index: number }>>();

  rawElements.forEach((el, index) => {
    const page = typeof el.page === "number" ? el.page : 1;
    if (!rawByPage.has(page)) rawByPage.set(page, []);
    rawByPage.get(page)!.push({ el, index });
  });

  sanitizedElements.forEach((el, index) => {
    const page = typeof el.page === "number" ? el.page : 1;
    if (!sanitizedByPage.has(page)) sanitizedByPage.set(page, []);
    sanitizedByPage.get(page)!.push({ el, index });
  });

  for (const [page, rawItems] of rawByPage.entries()) {
    const sanItems = sanitizedByPage.get(page) || [];

    for (const rawItem of rawItems) {
      const rawEl = rawItem.el;
      const rawText = normalizeText(rawEl.text);
      const key = rawEl.elementid || `raw-${page}-${rawItem.index}`;

      if (rawEl.elementid && sanitizedById.has(rawEl.elementid)) {
        const matched = sanitizedById.get(rawEl.elementid)!;
        const sanEl = matched.el;
        usedSanitizedIndexes.add(matched.index);

        const afterText = normalizeText(sanEl.text);

        rows.push({
          key,
          page,
          beforeText: rawText,
          afterText,
          changed: rawText !== afterText,
          removed: !!rawText && !afterText,
          added: false,
          matchType: "id",
        });
        continue;
      }

      let bestIndex = -1;
      let bestScore = 0;

      for (const sanItem of sanItems) {
        if (usedSanitizedIndexes.has(sanItem.index)) continue;

        const sanText = normalizeText(sanItem.el.text);
        if (!rawText && !sanText) continue;

        const score = textSimilarity(rawText, sanText);
        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          bestIndex = sanItem.index;
        }
      }

      if (bestIndex >= 0 && bestScore >= 0.42) {
        usedSanitizedIndexes.add(bestIndex);
        const sanEl = sanitizedElements[bestIndex];
        const afterText = normalizeText(sanEl.text);

        rows.push({
          key,
          page,
          beforeText: rawText,
          afterText,
          changed: rawText !== afterText,
          removed: !!rawText && !afterText,
          added: false,
          matchType: "heuristic",
        });
      } else {
        rows.push({
          key,
          page,
          beforeText: rawText,
          afterText: "",
          changed: true,
          removed: true,
          added: false,
          matchType: "removed",
        });
      }
    }
  }

  sanitizedElements.forEach((sanEl, index) => {
    if (usedSanitizedIndexes.has(index)) return;

    const page = typeof sanEl.page === "number" ? sanEl.page : 1;

    rows.push({
      key: sanEl.elementid || `san-${page}-${index}`,
      page,
      beforeText: "",
      afterText: normalizeText(sanEl.text),
      changed: true,
      removed: false,
      added: true,
      matchType: "added",
    });
  });

  return rows.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.page !== b.page) return a.page - b.page;
    return a.key.localeCompare(b.key);
  });
}

function groupRowsByPage(rows: CompareRow[]) {
  const map = new Map<number, CompareRow[]>();

  for (const row of rows) {
    if (!map.has(row.page)) map.set(row.page, []);
    map.get(row.page)!.push(row);
  }

  return Array.from(map.entries())
    .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])
    .map(([page, items]) => ({ page, items }));
}

function buildWordDiff(beforeText: string, afterText: string): DiffToken[] {
  if (!beforeText) return [];
  if (!afterText) return [{ type: "changed", text: beforeText }];

  const beforeTokens = tokenizeWithSpaces(beforeText);
  const afterTokens = tokenizeWithSpaces(afterText);

  const afterWordsNormalized = afterTokens
    .filter((t) => !isWhitespace(t))
    .map(normalizeWordForDiff)
    .filter(Boolean);

  const afterWordCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const token of afterWordsNormalized) {
    afterWordCounts.set(token, (afterWordCounts.get(token) || 0) + 1);
  }

  const seenBeforeCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  const result: DiffToken[] = [];

  for (const token of beforeTokens) {
    if (isWhitespace(token)) {
      result.push({ type: "space", text: token });
      continue;
    }

    const normalized = normalizeWordForDiff(token);

    if (!normalized) {
      result.push({ type: "same", text: token });
      continue;
    }

    const seen = (seenBeforeCounts.get(normalized) || 0) + 1;
    seenBeforeCounts.set(normalized, seen);

    const available = afterWordCounts.get(normalized) || 0;

    if (seen <= available) {
      result.push({ type: "same", text: token });
    } else {
      result.push({ type: "changed", text: token });
    }
  }

  return result;
}

function MatchBadge({ type }: { type: CompareRow["matchType"] }) {
  const config =
    type === "id"
      ? {
          cls: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-200",
          text: "match: id",
        }
      : type === "heuristic"
        ? {
            cls: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-200",
            text: "match: similar text",
          }
        : type === "removed"
          ? {
              cls: "bg-red-100 text-red-700 border-red-200",
              text: "removed",
            }
          : {
              cls: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200",
              text: "added",
            };

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-[10px] ${config.cls}`}>
      {config.text}
    </span>
  );
}

function BeforeTextDiff({
  beforeText,
  afterText,
  removed,
}: {
  beforeText: string;
  afterText: string;
  removed: boolean;
}) {
  if (!beforeText) {
    return <span className="text-gray-400 italic">Нет текста</span>;
  }

  if (removed) {
    return (
      <span className="rounded px-1 py-0.5 bg-red-100 text-red-900">
        {beforeText}
      </span>
    );
  }

  const tokens = buildWordDiff(beforeText, afterText);

  return (
    <>
      {tokens.map((token, index) => {
        if (token.type === "space") {
          return <React.Fragment key={index}>{token.text}</React.Fragment>;
        }

        if (token.type === "changed") {
          return (
            <span
              key={index}
              className="rounded px-1 py-0.5 bg-red-100 text-red-900"
            >
              {token.text}
            </span>
          );
        }

        return <React.Fragment key={index}>{token.text}</React.Fragment>;
      })}
    </>
  );
}

export const LayoutDebugModal: React.FC<Props> = ({ docId, isOpen, onClose }) => {
  /**
   * Важно:
   * backend endpoint /documents/{docId}/debug-layouts по умолчанию НЕ включает raw/sanitized JSON,
   * если не передать includeraw=true и includesanitized=true.
   *
   * Этот вызов предполагает, что хук useDocumentLayoutsDebug внутри себя делает запрос именно так.
   * Если хук этого не делает — ниже в UI ты увидишь "Нет данных для сравнения".
   */
  const {
    data,
    isLoading,
    error,
  } = useDocumentLayoutsDebug(isOpen ? docId : null, isOpen) as {
    data?: LayoutsDebugResponse;
    isLoading: boolean;
    error?: unknown;
  };

  const leftRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const rightRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const syncLockRef = useRef<"left" | "right" | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  const rawLayout = useMemo(() => extractRawLayout(data), [data]);
  const sanitizedLayout = useMemo(() => extractSanitizedLayout(data), [data]);

  const groupedPages = useMemo(() => {
    const rows = buildCompareRows(rawLayout, sanitizedLayout);
    return groupRowsByPage(rows);
  }, [rawLayout, sanitizedLayout]);

  const handleSyncScroll = (source: "left" | "right") => {
    const left = leftRef.current;
    const right = rightRef.current;
    if (!left || !right) return;

    if (syncLockRef.current && syncLockRef.current !== source) return;

    syncLockRef.current = source;

    if (source === "left") {
      right.scrollTop = left.scrollTop;
    } else {
      left.scrollTop = right.scrollTop;
    }

    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      syncLockRef.current = null;
    });
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleBackdropClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
  };

  const errorMessage =
    error instanceof Error ? error.message : error ? String(error) : "Неизвестная ошибка";

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
      onClick={handleBackdropClick}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="layout-compare-v3-title"
        className="w-full max-w-[96vw] h-[94vh] rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 border-b bg-gray-50">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h2
              id="layout-compare-v3-title"
              className="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900"
            >
              Документ до и после санитизации
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 break-all">
              В левой колонке подсвечены только изменившиеся слова
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 rounded-md text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200 hover:text-gray-700"
            aria-label="Закрыть"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-5 py-3 border-b bg-white text-sm text-gray-700">
          Слева исходный текст, справа санитизированный. Если строка изменилась, в левой колонке подсвечиваются только изменённые слова; если строка удалена полностью, подсвечивается вся строка.
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 bg-gray-100">
          {isLoading && (
            <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500 bg-white">
              <Loader2 className="animate-spin mr-2" size={18} />
              Загрузка сравнения…
            </div>
          )}

          {!!error && !isLoading && (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center bg-white">
              <AlertTriangle size={20} className="mb-2 text-red-500" />
              <div className="text-sm font-medium text-red-700">
                Ошибка загрузки данных
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500 break-all max-w-2xl">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!isLoading && !error && groupedPages.length === 0 && (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center text-sm text-gray-500 bg-white px-6">
              <div>Нет данных для сравнения raw / sanitized layout.</div>
              <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-400">
                Проверь, что запрос debug-layouts вызывается с includeraw=true и includesanitized=true.
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!isLoading && !error && groupedPages.length > 0 && (
            <div className="h-full grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-px bg-gray-200">
              <div className="min-h-0 bg-white flex flex-col">
                <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 px-4 py-3 border-b bg-white">
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                    До санитизации
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div
                  ref={leftRef}
                  onScroll={() => handleSyncScroll("left")}
                  className="flex-1 overflow-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-6"
                >
                  {groupedPages.map((page) => (
                    <section key={`left-page-${page.page}`} className="space-y-3">
                      <div className="sticky top-0 z-[1] inline-flex text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border border-gray-200">
                        Страница {page.page}
                      </div>

                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        {page.items.map((row) => (
                          <div
                            key={`left-${row.key}`}
                            className="rounded-md px-3 py-2 border border-transparent"
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                              <MatchBadge type={row.matchType} />
                            </div>

                            <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-gray-900">
                              <BeforeTextDiff
                                beforeText={row.beforeText}
                                afterText={row.afterText}
                                removed={row.removed}
                              />
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </section>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="min-h-0 bg-white flex flex-col">
                <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 px-4 py-3 border-b bg-white">
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                    После санитизации
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div
                  ref={rightRef}
                  onScroll={() => handleSyncScroll("right")}
                  className="flex-1 overflow-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-6"
                >
                  {groupedPages.map((page) => (
                    <section key={`right-page-${page.page}`} className="space-y-3">
                      <div className="sticky top-0 z-[1] inline-flex text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border border-gray-200">
                        Страница {page.page}
                      </div>

                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        {page.items.map((row) => (
                          <div
                            key={`right-${row.key}`}
                            className={
                              row.added
                                ? "rounded-md border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2"
                                : "rounded-md border border-transparent bg-transparent px-3 py-2"
                            }
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                              <MatchBadge type={row.matchType} />
                            </div>

                            <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-gray-900">
                              {row.removed ? (
                                <span className="text-gray-400 italic">
                                  Удалено после санитизации
                                </span>
                              ) : row.afterText ? (
                                row.afterText
                              ) : (
                                <span className="text-gray-400 italic">Нет текста</span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </section>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="px-5 py-3 border-t bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-end">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-md bg-gray-900 text-white hover:bg-gray-800"
          >
            Закрыть
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LayoutDebugModal;

import React, {
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import type {
  ChunkPreviewDTO,
  ChunksPreviewDTO,
  PipelineStageViewDTO,
} from "../../../types";

interface Props {
  stage: PipelineStageViewDTO;
  highlightedChunkIndices?: number[];
  traceLabel?: string | null;
}

type RenderSegment =
  | {
      type: "plain";
      key: string;
      text: string;
    }
  | {
      type: "highlight";
      key: string;
      text: string;
      chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO;
    };

type ChunkGroupKey = "sections" | "clauses" | "subclauses";

interface ChunkGroup {
  key: ChunkGroupKey;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  items: ChunkPreviewDTO[];
}

interface SegmentGroup {
  segmentId: string;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  segmentType: string | null;
  isPrimary: boolean;
  order: number | null;
  items: ChunkPreviewDTO[];
}

function cx(...classes: Array<string | false | null | undefined>): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

function isChunksPreviewDTO(value: unknown): value is ChunksPreviewDTO {
  return Boolean(
    value &&
      typeof value === "object" &&
      "chunks" in (value as Record<string, unknown>) &&
      Array.isArray((value as Record<string, unknown>).chunks),
  );
}

function normalizeChunkText(text: unknown): string {
  return typeof text === "string" ? text : "";
}

function pageLabel(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): string {
  const from = typeof chunk.page_from === "number" ? chunk.page_from : null;
  const to =
    typeof chunk.page_to === "number"
      ? chunk.page_to
      : typeof chunk.page_from === "number"
        ? chunk.page_from
        : null;

  if (from == null && to == null) return "—";
  if (from != null && to != null) {
    return from === to ? `${from}` : `${from}–${to}`;
  }
  return String(from ?? to ?? "—");
}

function chunkTitle(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): string {
  return `Чанк #${chunk.index}`;
}

function chunkMetaLine(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  const pages = pageLabel(chunk);

  if (pages !== "—") {
    parts.push(`стр. ${pages}`);
  }

  if (chunk.clause_uid) {
    parts.push(`clause_uid: ${chunk.clause_uid}`);
  }

  if (chunk.segment_type) {
    parts.push(
      `segment: ${chunk.segment_type}${
        chunk.is_primary_segment ? " (PRIMARY)" : ""
      }`,
    );
  }

  return parts.length ? parts.join(" · ") : "Без метаданных";
}

function fallbackReconstructedText(chunks: ChunkPreviewDTO[]): string {
  return chunks
    .map((chunk) => normalizeChunkText(chunk.text).trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n\n");
}

function stageRawJson(preview: ChunksPreviewDTO): string {
  const raw = preview.raw_json ?? { chunks: preview.chunks };
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(raw, null, 2);
  } catch {
    return JSON.stringify({ chunks: preview.chunks }, null, 2);
  }
}

function chunkJson(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(chunk, null, 2);
  } catch {
    return JSON.stringify({ index: chunk.index }, null, 2);
  }
}

function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
}

/**
 * Подсветка фрагмента текста chunk’а внутри snapshot JSON.
 * Ищем первые N символов текста chunk’а как сырую подстроку.
 */
function buildHighlightedSnapshot(
  preview: ChunksPreviewDTO,
  selectedChunk: ChunkPreviewDTO | undefined,
): { __html: string } {
  const raw = stageRawJson(preview);
  const chunkTextFull = selectedChunk
    ? normalizeChunkText(selectedChunk.text).trim()
    : "";

  if (!chunkTextFull) {
    return { __html: escapeHtml(raw) };
  }

  // Нормализуем пробелы, чтобы чуть повысить шанс совпадения
  const normalized = chunkTextFull.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const snippet = normalized.slice(0, 80);
  if (!snippet) {
    return { __html: escapeHtml(raw) };
  }

  let idx = raw.indexOf(snippet);
  if (idx === -1 && snippet.length > 30) {
    // Fallback: попробуем более короткий кусок
    const shorter = snippet.slice(0, 30);
    idx = raw.indexOf(shorter);
    if (idx === -1) {
      return { __html: escapeHtml(raw) };
    }
  } else if (idx === -1) {
    return { __html: escapeHtml(raw) };
  }

  const before = escapeHtml(raw.slice(0, idx));
  const middle = escapeHtml(raw.slice(idx, idx + snippet.length));
  const after = escapeHtml(raw.slice(idx + snippet.length));

  return {
    __html: `${before}<mark data-snapshot-chunk="true">${middle}</mark>${after}`,
  };
}

/* ===== ГРУППИРОВКА CHUNKS: sections / clauses / subclauses ===== */

function getClauseDepthFromString(value: string | null | undefined): number {
  const trimmed = (value ?? "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return 0;
  return trimmed.split(".").filter(Boolean).length;
}

function getChunkClauseDepth(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): number {
  // Основной источник — clause_uid, но если его нет, глубина 0 (section‑подобный чанк).
  return getClauseDepthFromString(chunk.clause_uid);
}

function getChunkGroupKey(chunk: ChunkPreviewDTO): ChunkGroupKey {
  const depth = getChunkClauseDepth(chunk);

  if (depth === 0) return "sections";
  if (depth <= 2) return "clauses";
  return "subclauses";
}

function groupChunks(chunks: ChunkPreviewDTO[]): ChunkGroup[] {
  const sections: ChunkPreviewDTO[] = [];
  const clauses: ChunkPreviewDTO[] = [];
  const subclauses: ChunkPreviewDTO[] = [];

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const key = getChunkGroupKey(chunk);
    if (key === "sections") {
      sections.push(chunk);
    } else if (key === "clauses") {
      clauses.push(chunk);
    } else {
      subclauses.push(chunk);
    }
  }

  // Можно слегка отсортировать внутри групп по index
  sections.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
  clauses.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
  subclauses.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);

  const result: ChunkGroup[] = [];

  result.push({
    key: "sections",
    label: "Sections",
    description: "Крупные section‑подобные чанки (без clause_uid).",
    items: sections,
  });

  result.push({
    key: "clauses",
    label: "Clauses",
    description:
      "Обычные materialized clauses (clause_uid глубиной 1–2, напр. 2, 2.3, 4.7).",
    items: clauses,
  });

  result.push({
    key: "subclauses",
    label: "Subclauses",
    description:
      "Более глубокие подпункты (clause_uid глубиной 3+ — 2.2.10, 3.1.1, 6.1.5).",
    items: subclauses,
  });

  return result;
}

/* ===== ГРУППИРОВКА CHUNKS ПО СЕГМЕНТАМ (v2) ===== */

function groupChunksBySegment(preview: ChunksPreviewDTO | null): SegmentGroup[] {
  if (!preview || !Array.isArray(preview.segments) || preview.segments.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  const result: SegmentGroup[] = [];

  for (const seg of preview.segments) {
    if (!seg || !seg.segment || !Array.isArray(seg.chunks) || seg.chunks.length === 0) {
      continue;
    }

    const s = seg.segment;
    const segmentId = s.segment_id;
    const title = s.segment_title || "(без заголовка)";
    const type = s.segment_type || "primary";
    const order = typeof s.segment_order === "number" ? s.segment_order : null;
    const isPrimary = !!s.is_primary;

    const labelParts: string[] = [];
    if (order != null) labelParts.push(`#${order}`);
    labelParts.push(title);
    labelParts.push(type);
    if (isPrimary) labelParts.push("PRIMARY");

    result.push({
      segmentId,
      label: labelParts.join(" · "),
      description: `Сегмент ${segmentId}`,
      segmentType: s.segment_type ?? null,
      isPrimary,
      order,
      items: seg.chunks.filter(Boolean),
    });
  }

  // Сортируем сегменты: primary выше, затем по order
  result.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.isPrimary && !b.isPrimary) return -1;
    if (!a.isPrimary && b.isPrimary) return 1;
    const ao = a.order ?? 0;
    const bo = b.order ?? 0;
    return ao - bo;
  });

  return result;
}

export const ChunksStageViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  stage,
  highlightedChunkIndices = [],
  traceLabel = null,
}) => {
  const status = stage.status;

  // V2_ONLY: сначала честно обрабатываем status.
  if (status === "pending") {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Чанки ещё не готовы в v2-формате. Попробуй позже.
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (status === "missing") {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
        CHUNKS-артефакт ожидается, но недоступен или повреждён.
        Проверь состояние пайплайна для стадии CHUNKS.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const preview = useMemo(
    () => (isChunksPreviewDTO(stage.preview) ? stage.preview : null),
    [stage.preview],
  );

  const isSourcePaneMode =
    highlightedChunkIndices.length > 0 || traceLabel != null;


  const allChunks = useMemo<ChunkPreviewDTO[]>(() => {
    if (!preview) return [];

    if (Array.isArray(preview.segments) && preview.segments.length > 0) {
      const fromSegments: ChunkPreviewDTO[] = [];
      for (const seg of preview.segments) {
        if (!seg || !Array.isArray(seg.chunks)) continue;
        for (const ch of seg.chunks) {
          if (ch) fromSegments.push(ch);
        }
      }
      return fromSegments;
    }

    // V2_ONLY: если сегментов нет, считаем, что canonical corpus отсутствует.
    return [];
  }, [preview]);








  const hasChunks = allChunks.length > 0;

  const chunkGroups = useMemo<ChunkGroup[]>(
    () => groupChunks(allChunks),
    [allChunks],
  );

  const segmentGroups = useMemo<SegmentGroup[]>(
    () => groupChunksBySegment(preview),
    [preview],
  );

  const hasSegmentGroups = segmentGroups.length > 0;

  const highlightedSet = useMemo(
    () => new Set<number>(highlightedChunkIndices),
    [highlightedChunkIndices],
  );

  const highlightedChunks = useMemo(
    () => allChunks.filter((chunk) => highlightedSet.has(chunk.index)),
    [allChunks, highlightedSet],
  );

  const hasLinkedChunks = highlightedChunks.length > 0;

  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [jsonMode, setJsonMode] = useState<"chunk" | "snapshot">("chunk");

  const listItemRefs = useRef<Map<number, HTMLButtonElement>>(new Map());
  const inlineHighlightRefs = useRef<Map<number, HTMLButtonElement>>(new Map());
  const snapshotRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  // Состояние развёрнутости групп в левой колонке (legacy depth-группы)
  const [expandedGroups, setExpandedGroups] = useState<
    Record<ChunkGroupKey, boolean>
  >({
    sections: false,
    clauses: true,
    subclauses: false,
  });

  const toggleGroup = (key: ChunkGroupKey) => {
    setExpandedGroups((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }));
  };

  // Состояние развёрнутости сегментов (v2)
  const [expandedSegments, setExpandedSegments] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(
    {},
  );

  const toggleSegment = (segmentId: string) => {
    setExpandedSegments((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [segmentId]: !prev[segmentId],
    }));
  };

  // Инициализация выбранного чанка
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasChunks) {
      setSelectedIndex(null);
      return;
    }

    if (isSourcePaneMode) {
      setSelectedIndex((prev) => {
        if (prev != null && highlightedSet.has(prev)) return prev;
        return highlightedChunks[0]?.index ?? null;
      });
      return;
    }

    setSelectedIndex((prev) => {
      if (prev != null && allChunks.some((chunk) => chunk.index === prev)) {
        return prev;
      }
      return allChunks[0]?.index ?? null;
    });
  }, [allChunks, hasChunks, highlightedChunks, highlightedSet, isSourcePaneMode]);

  // Скролл к элементу в списке/inline подсветке
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedIndex == null) return;

    const listButton = listItemRefs.current.get(selectedIndex);
    if (listButton) {
      listButton.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", behavior: "smooth" });
      return;
    }

    const inlineButton = inlineHighlightRefs.current.get(selectedIndex);
    if (inlineButton) {
      inlineButton.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }, [selectedIndex]);

  const selectedChunk = useMemo(
    () =>
      selectedIndex == null
        ? undefined
        : allChunks.find((c) => c.index === selectedIndex),
    [allChunks, selectedIndex],
  );

  const reconstructedText = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = preview?.reconstructed_text;
    if (typeof raw === "string" && raw.trim()) return raw;
    return fallbackReconstructedText(allChunks);
  }, [preview, allChunks]);

  const renderSegments = useMemo<RenderSegment[]>(() => {
    if (!hasChunks) return [];
    return allChunks.map((chunk) => {
      const text = normalizeChunkText(chunk.text);
      if (highlightedSet.has(chunk.index)) {
        return {
          type: "highlight",
          key: `highlight-${chunk.index}`,
          text,
          chunk,
        };
      }
      return {
        type: "plain",
        key: `plain-${chunk.index}`,
        text,
      };
    });
  }, [allChunks, hasChunks, highlightedSet]);

  const highlightedSnapshot = useMemo(
    () =>
      preview ? buildHighlightedSnapshot(preview, selectedChunk) : { __html: "" },
    [preview, selectedChunk],
  );

  // Скролл к mark после обновления DOM с новым snapshot’ом
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!snapshotRef.current || jsonMode !== "snapshot") return;
    const mark = snapshotRef.current.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
      "mark[data-snapshot-chunk='true']",
    );
    if (mark) {
      mark.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }, [jsonMode, highlightedSnapshot.__html, selectedChunk?.index]);

  if (!preview) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        CHUNKS preview недоступен для этого этапа.
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* SOURCE-PANE MODE */
  if (isSourcePaneMode) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-3">
        {traceLabel && (
          <div className="rounded-md border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-blue-700">
            {traceLabel}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Исходный текст документа
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
                {hasLinkedChunks
                  ? "Связанные с выбранной секцией чанки подсвечены inline."
                  : "Для выбранной секции не найдено связанных chunk’ов."}
              </p>
            </div>

            {hasLinkedChunks && (
              <div className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-amber-700">
                Подсвечено: {highlightedChunks.length}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-md border border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-4">
            {hasChunks ? (
              <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">
                {renderSegments.map((segment) => {
                  if (segment.type === "highlight") {
                    const isSelected = selectedIndex === segment.chunk.index;
                    const titleParts = [
                      chunkTitle(segment.chunk),
                      `стр. ${pageLabel(segment.chunk)}`,
                      segment.chunk.clause_uid
                        ? `clause_uid: ${segment.chunk.clause_uid}`
                        : null,
                    ].filter(Boolean);

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={segment.key}
                        ref={(el) => {
                          if (el) {
                            inlineHighlightRefs.current.set(
                              segment.chunk.index,
                              el,
                            );
                          } else {
                            inlineHighlightRefs.current.delete(
                              segment.chunk.index,
                            );
                          }
                        }}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setSelectedIndex(segment.chunk.index)}
                        className={cx(
                          "inline whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-left transition-colors",
                          isSelected
                            ? "bg-amber-300/80 ring-1 ring-amber-500"
                            : "bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200",
                        )}
                        title={titleParts.join(" · ")}
                      >
                        {segment.text || " "}
                      </button>
                    );
                  }

                  return (
                    <React.Fragment key={segment.key}>
                      {segment.text}
                    </React.Fragment>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                Исходный текст документа недоступен.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 min-h-[1.5rem]">
            {selectedChunk ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                Активная подсветка: {chunkTitle(selectedChunk)}
                {pageLabel(selectedChunk) !== "—" &&
                  ` · страницы: ${pageLabel(selectedChunk)}`}
                {selectedChunk.clause_uid &&
                  ` · clause_uid: ${selectedChunk.clause_uid}`}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                Нет активного chunk’а для подсветки.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* CANONICAL CHUNKS VIEW */
  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[25%_30%_45%]">
      {/* Левая колонка: список чанков, приоритет — сегменты v2 */}
      <aside className="flex min-h-0 flex-col rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white">
        <div className="border-b bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
            CHUNKS
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
            Материализованные чанки этапа, сгруппированные по сегментам или
            уровню.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          {hasChunks ? (
            <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
              {hasSegmentGroups
                ? segmentGroups.map((group) => {
                    const isExpanded =
                      expandedSegments[group.segmentId] ?? true;
                    const count = group.items.length;
                    if (count === 0) return null;

                    return (
                      <div key={group.segmentId}>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => toggleSegment(group.segmentId)}
                          className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500"
                        >
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span
                              className={cx(
                                "inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded border border-gray-300 bg-white text-[9px] leading-none text-gray-600",
                              )}
                            >
                              {isExpanded ? "−" : "+"}
                            </span>
                            {group.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-gray-400">
                            {count}
                          </span>
                        </button>

                        {isExpanded && (
                          <div className="border-t border-gray-100">
                            {group.items.map((chunk) => {
                              const isSelected = selectedIndex === chunk.index;
                              return (
                                <button
                                  key={chunk.index}
                                  ref={(el) => {
                                    if (el) {
                                      listItemRefs.current.set(chunk.index, el);
                                    } else {
                                      listItemRefs.current.delete(chunk.index);
                                    }
                                  }}
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => setSelectedIndex(chunk.index)}
                                  className={cx(
                                    "w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs transition-colors",
                                    isSelected
                                      ? "bg-blue-50"
                                      : "bg-white hover:bg-gray-50",
                                  )}
                                  aria-pressed={isSelected}
                                  title={chunkMetaLine(chunk)}
                                >
                                  <div className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-800">
                                    {chunkTitle(chunk)}
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                                    {chunkMetaLine(chunk)}
                                  </div>
                                </button>
                              );
                            })}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })
                : chunkGroups.map((group) => {
                    const isExpanded = expandedGroups[group.key];
                    const count = group.items.length;

                    if (count === 0) {
                      return null;
                    }

                    return (
                      <div key={group.key}>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => toggleGroup(group.key)}
                          className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500"
                        >
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span
                              className={cx(
                                "inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded border border-gray-300 bg-white text-[9px] leading-none text-gray-600",
                              )}
                            >
                              {isExpanded ? "−" : "+"}
                            </span>
                            {group.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-gray-400">
                            {count}
                          </span>
                        </button>

                        {isExpanded && (
                          <div className="border-t border-gray-100">
                            {group.items.map((chunk) => {
                              const isSelected = selectedIndex === chunk.index;

                              return (
                                <button
                                  key={chunk.index}
                                  ref={(el) => {
                                    if (el) {
                                      listItemRefs.current.set(chunk.index, el);
                                    } else {
                                      listItemRefs.current.delete(chunk.index);
                                    }
                                  }}
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => setSelectedIndex(chunk.index)}
                                  className={cx(
                                    "w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs transition-colors",
                                    isSelected
                                      ? "bg-blue-50"
                                      : "bg-white hover:bg-gray-50",
                                  )}
                                  aria-pressed={isSelected}
                                  title={chunkMetaLine(chunk)}
                                >
                                  <div className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-800">
                                    {chunkTitle(chunk)}
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                                    {chunkMetaLine(chunk)}
                                  </div>
                                </button>
                              );
                            })}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="px-3 py-3 text-sm text-gray-500">
              В v2-артефакте CHUNKS нет сегментов или чанков.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Средняя колонка: выбранный chunk */}
      <section className="min-h-0 min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
        <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <div className="mb-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
              Выбранный chunk
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
              Canonical‑представление materialized chunks.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
            {selectedChunk ? (
              <div className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">
                    {chunkTitle(selectedChunk)}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                    {chunkMetaLine(selectedChunk)}
                  </p>

                  <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                    <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1">
                      index: {selectedChunk.index}
                    </span>

                    {selectedChunk.clause_uid && (
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1">
                        clause_uid: {selectedChunk.clause_uid}
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {pageLabel(selectedChunk) !== "—" && (
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1">
                        pages: {pageLabel(selectedChunk)}
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {selectedChunk.segment_type && (
                      <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1">
                        segment: {selectedChunk.segment_type}
                        {selectedChunk.is_primary_segment ? " (PRIMARY)" : ""}
                      </span>
                    )}

                    <span className="rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1">
                      chars: {normalizeChunkText(selectedChunk.text).length}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
                  <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                    Текст chunk’а
                  </p>
                  <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words font-sans text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">
                    {normalizeChunkText(selectedChunk.text) || "—"}
                  </pre>
                </div>

                {reconstructedText ? (
                  <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
                    <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                      Контекст stage
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-gray-500">
                      Справа можно переключаться между JSON выбранного chunk’а и
                      полным snapshot этапа, прокручиваемым к этому chunk’у.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
                Выбери chunk слева.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Правая колонка: JSON chunk’а / JSON snapshot с переключателем */}
      <section className="min-h-0 min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
        <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
          <div className="mb-3 flex min-h-[3.5rem] items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                {jsonMode === "chunk" ? "JSON chunk’а" : "JSON snapshot"}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-gray-400">
                {jsonMode === "chunk"
                  ? "Текущий объект materialized chunk’а из snapshot CHUNKS."
                  : "Полный canonical snapshot CHUNKS; выделен фрагмент текста выбранного chunk’а."}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setJsonMode("chunk")}
                  className={cx(
                    "rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[11px] leading-none transition-colors",
                    jsonMode === "chunk"
                      ? "bg-gray-900 text-gray-100"
                      : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900",
                  )}
                >
                  chunk
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setJsonMode("snapshot")}
                  className={cx(
                    "rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[11px] leading-none transition-colors",
                    jsonMode === "snapshot"
                      ? "bg-gray-900 text-gray-100"
                      : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900",
                  )}
                >
                  snapshot
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {jsonMode === "chunk" ? (
            selectedChunk ? (
              <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-gray-950 p-3">
                <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs leading-5 text-gray-100">
                  {chunkJson(selectedChunk)}
                </pre>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
                Выбери chunk слева.
              </div>
            )
          ) : (
            <div
              ref={snapshotRef}
              className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-gray-950 p-3"
            >
              <pre
                className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs leading-5 text-gray-100"
                dangerouslySetInnerHTML={highlightedSnapshot}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ChunksStageViewer;

import React from "react";
import type {
  DocumentPipelineViewResponseDTO,
  PipelineStageViewDTO,
} from "../../../types";
import { getDocumentPipelineView } from "../../../api/documentPipelineView";
import { PipelineStageRow } from "./PipelineStageRow";
import { PipelineStageModal } from "./PipelineStageModal";

interface Props {
  documentId: string;
}

function getErrorMessage(err: unknown): string {
  if (typeof err === "object" && err !== null) {
    const maybeDetail = (err as { detail?: unknown }).detail;
    if (typeof maybeDetail === "string" && maybeDetail.trim()) {
      return maybeDetail;
    }

    const maybeMessage = (err as { message?: unknown }).message;
    if (typeof maybeMessage === "string" && maybeMessage.trim()) {
      return maybeMessage;
    }
  }

  return "Не удалось загрузить стадии документа";
}

export const DocumentPipelinePanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ documentId }) => {
  const [data, setData] =
    React.useState<DocumentPipelineViewResponseDTO | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedStage, setSelectedStage] =
    React.useState<PipelineStageViewDTO | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function load() {
      if (!documentId) {
        setData(null);
        setSelectedStage(null);
        setError("Не указан идентификатор документа");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        setData(null);
        setSelectedStage(null);

        // Панель всегда загружает pipeline в canonical-режиме по умолчанию.
        // Переключение в friendly будет происходить внутри modal конкретной стадии.
        const result = await getDocumentPipelineView(documentId, controller.signal);

        setData(result);
      } catch (err) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) {
          return;
        }

        setError(getErrorMessage(err));
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void load();

    return () => {
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [documentId]);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-1">
            SOURCE → PRIMARY → EXTRACT → PII → STRUCTURE → CHUNKS
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
            Здесь отображается путь документа по стадиям обработки — от
            исходного PDF до структуры и чанков.
          </p>
        </div>

        {loading && (
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
            Загрузка стадий обработки…
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && error && (
          <div className="rounded border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && !error && data && data.stages.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {data.stages.map((stage) => (
              <PipelineStageRow
                key={stage.code}
                stage={stage}
                onOpen={setSelectedStage}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && !error && data && data.stages.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
            Стадии обработки пока недоступны.
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <PipelineStageModal
        stage={selectedStage}
        stages={data?.stages ?? []}
        documentId={documentId}
        onClose={() => setSelectedStage(null)}
      />
    </>
  );
};

export default DocumentPipelinePanel;

>>> FILE_START | index=37 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/documents/pipeline/LayoutStageViewer.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import type { LayoutPreviewDTO, PipelineStageViewDTO } from "../../../types";

interface Props {
  stage: PipelineStageViewDTO;
}

/**
 * LayoutStageViewer
 *
 * Показывает layout-артефакт в двух вкладках:
 * - Просмотр: страницы и извлечённый текст;
 * - JSON: сырой payload артефакта.
 *
 * Название и описание стадии уже показаны в шапке модалки,
 * поэтому здесь мы не дублируем их повторно.
 */
export const LayoutStageViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({ stage }) => {
  const preview = stage.preview as LayoutPreviewDTO | null | undefined;
  const [tab, setTab] = React.useState<"view" | "json">("view");

  if (!preview) {
    return (
      <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
        Layout-превью недоступно.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const jsonText = JSON.stringify(preview.raw_json ?? null, null, 2);

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setTab("view")}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs border ${
            tab === "view"
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600"
              : "bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200"
          }`}
        >
          Просмотр
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setTab("json")}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs border ${
            tab === "json"
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600"
              : "bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200"
          }`}
        >
          JSON
        </button>
      </div>

      {tab === "view" ? (
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
          {preview.pages.length > 0 ? (
            preview.pages.map((page) => (
              <div
                key={page.page}
                className="border border-gray-100 rounded-lg p-3 bg-gray-50"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-2">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-800">
                    Страница {page.page}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                    Элементов: {page.element_count}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs leading-5 text-gray-700 font-sans">
                  {page.text || "—"}
                </pre>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              Нет страниц для отображения.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <pre className="flex-1 overflow-auto text-xs leading-5 bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4">
          {jsonText}
        </pre>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default LayoutStageViewer;

>>> FILE_START | index=38 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/documents/pipeline/PdfStageViewer.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import type { PdfPreviewDTO, PipelineStageViewDTO } from "../../../types";

interface Props {
  stage: PipelineStageViewDTO;
}

/**
 * PdfStageViewer
 *
 * Показывает PDF-артефакт стадии:
 * - если есть inline_url — в iframe;
 * - если нет — сообщение, что встроенный просмотр недоступен;
 * - всегда оставляет ссылку "Открыть в новой вкладке", если есть download_url.
 *
 * Название/описание стадии рендерятся в шапке модалки,
 * поэтому здесь они не дублируются.
 */
export const PdfStageViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({ stage }) => {
  const preview = stage.preview as PdfPreviewDTO | null | undefined;

  if (!preview) {
    return (
      <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
        PDF-превью недоступно.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
          {/* Небольшая подсказка вместо повторения title/description */}
          Исходный PDF этого этапа.
        </div>

        {preview.download_url && (
          <a
            href={preview.download_url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded border text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-50"
          >
            Открыть в новой вкладке
          </a>
        )}
      </div>

      {preview.inline_url ? (
        <iframe
          title={`pipeline-stage-${stage.code}`}
          src={preview.inline_url}
          className="w-full flex-1 min-h-[70vh] border rounded-lg bg-white"
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
          PDF недоступен для встроенного просмотра.
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default PdfStageViewer;
