>>> FILE_START | index=39 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/documents/pipeline/PipelineStageModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import type {
  PipelineStageKind,
  PipelineStageViewDTO,
  StageViewMode,
} from "../../../types";
import { getDocumentPipelineView } from "../../../api/documentPipelineView";
import { PdfStageViewer } from "./PdfStageViewer";
import { LayoutStageViewer } from "./LayoutStageViewer";
import { StructureStageViewer } from "./StructureStageViewer";
import { ChunksStageViewer } from "./ChunksStageViewer";
import { StageRepresentationBanner } from "./StageRepresentationBanner";

interface Props {
  stage: PipelineStageViewDTO | null;
  stages: PipelineStageViewDTO[];
  documentId: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

// Список типов стадий, для которых реально есть смысл в friendly-режиме.
// Пока включаем только STRUCTURE, как обсуждали.
const FRIENDLY_SUPPORTED_KINDS: PipelineStageKind[] = ["structure"];

function supportsFriendlyMode(stage: PipelineStageViewDTO): boolean {
  return FRIENDLY_SUPPORTED_KINDS.includes(stage.kind);
}

function getErrorMessage(err: unknown): string {
  if (typeof err === "object" && err !== null) {
    const maybeDetail = (err as { detail?: unknown }).detail;
    if (typeof maybeDetail === "string" && maybeDetail.trim()) {
      return maybeDetail;
    }

    const maybeMessage = (err as { message?: unknown }).message;
    if (typeof maybeMessage === "string" && maybeMessage.trim()) {
      return maybeMessage;
    }
  }

  return "Не удалось переключить режим отображения стадии";
}

export const PipelineStageModal: React.FC<Props> = ({
  stage,
  stages,
  documentId,
  onClose,
}) => {
  // Текущая версия открытой стадии (может обновляться при переключении режима)
  const [currentStage, setCurrentStage] =
    React.useState<PipelineStageViewDTO | null>(stage);

  // Полный актуальный набор стадий для текущего view_mode.
  // Нужен, чтобы structure-viewer мог получить chunksStage.
  const [currentStages, setCurrentStages] =
    React.useState<PipelineStageViewDTO[]>(stages);

  // Локальный режим представления (canonical / friendly)
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = React.useState<StageViewMode>("canonical");

  // Статусы загрузки/ошибки при переключении режима
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  // При смене входного stage (клик по другой строке в списке)
  // переинициализируем локальное состояние.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentStage(stage || null);
    setCurrentStages(stages);
    setError(null);

    if (stage?.representation?.view_mode) {
      setViewMode(stage.representation.view_mode);
    } else {
      setViewMode("canonical");
    }
  }, [stage, stages]);

  // Если стадии нет, модалка не рендерится вообще.
  if (!currentStage) {
    return null;
  }

  const stageData: PipelineStageViewDTO = currentStage;
  const canToggleFriendly =
    supportsFriendlyMode(stageData) && stageData.status === "ready";

  async function handleToggleViewMode() {
    if (!documentId || !canToggleFriendly) return;

    const nextViewMode: StageViewMode =
      viewMode === "canonical" ? "friendly" : "canonical";

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const result = await getDocumentPipelineView(
        documentId,
        undefined,
        nextViewMode,
      );

      const updatedStage =
        result.stages.find((item) => item.code === stageData.code) ?? null;

      if (!updatedStage) {
        throw new Error(
          `Stage ${stageData.code} not found in pipeline response`,
        );
      }

      setCurrentStages(result.stages);
      setCurrentStage(updatedStage);
      setViewMode(nextViewMode);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(getErrorMessage(err));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function renderContent() {
    switch (stageData.kind) {
      case "pdf":
        return <PdfStageViewer stage={stageData} />;

      case "layout":
        return <LayoutStageViewer stage={stageData} />;

      case "structure": {
        const chunksStage =
          currentStages.find((item) => item.code === "CHUNKS") ?? null;

        return (
          <StructureStageViewer
            structureStage={stageData}
            chunksStage={chunksStage}
          />
        );
      }

      case "chunks":
        return <ChunksStageViewer stage={stageData} />;

      default:
        return (
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
            Для этой стадии пока нет viewer.
          </div>
        );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4">
      <div className="flex h-[90vh] w-full max-w-7xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-white shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b px-5 py-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
              {stageData.code}
            </p>
            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-gray-900">
              {stageData.title}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
              {stageData.description}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
            {canToggleFriendly && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleToggleViewMode}
                disabled={loading}
                className={`rounded border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition ${
                  loading
                    ? "cursor-not-allowed border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 text-gray-400"
                    : viewMode === "canonical"
                      ? "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100"
                      : "border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
                }`}
                title={
                  viewMode === "canonical"
                    ? "Показать более удобный для чтения вид"
                    : "Вернуться к каноническому результату этапа"
                }
              >
                {loading
                  ? "Переключение..."
                  : viewMode === "canonical"
                    ? "Показать friendly формат"
                    : "Показать canonical формат"}
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50"
            >
              Закрыть
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <StageRepresentationBanner
          representation={stageData.representation}
          meta={stageData.meta ?? null}
        />

        {error && (
          <div className="mx-5 mt-4 rounded border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-5">{renderContent()}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PipelineStageModal;

import React from "react";
import type { PipelineStageViewDTO } from "../../../types";
import { StatusBadge } from "./StatusBadge";

interface Props {
  stage: PipelineStageViewDTO;
  onOpen: (stage: PipelineStageViewDTO) => void;
}

function buildMetaLine(stage: PipelineStageViewDTO): string | null {
  const meta = stage.meta;
  if (!meta) return null;

  const parts: string[] = [];

  if (typeof meta.page_count === "number") {
    parts.push(`стр.: ${meta.page_count}`);
  }
  if (typeof meta.element_count === "number") {
    parts.push(`элементов: ${meta.element_count}`);
  }
  if (typeof meta.section_count === "number") {
    parts.push(`секций: ${meta.section_count}`);
  }
  if (typeof meta.chunk_count === "number") {
    parts.push(`чанков: ${meta.chunk_count}`);
  }
  if (meta.source_file_name) {
    parts.push(meta.source_file_name);
  }

  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(" · ") : null;
}

function getOpenDisabledReason(stage: PipelineStageViewDTO): string {
  if (stage.status === "pending") {
    return "Стадия ещё не готова";
  }

  if (stage.status === "missing") {
    return "Артефакт стадии недоступен или повреждён";
  }

  if (!stage.available) {
    return "Стадия недоступна для просмотра";
  }

  return "Стадия пока недоступна для просмотра";
}

export const PipelineStageRow: React.FC<Props> = ({ stage, onOpen }) => {
  const canOpen = stage.available && stage.status === "ready";
  const metaLine = buildMetaLine(stage);

  return (
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-100 bg-white p-3">
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
            {stage.code}
          </span>

          <StatusBadge status={stage.status} />
        </div>

        <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">{stage.title}</p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-gray-500">
          {stage.description}
        </p>

        {metaLine && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-gray-400">{metaLine}</p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="shrink-0">
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!canOpen}
          onClick={() => onOpen(stage)}
          title={canOpen ? "Открыть стадию" : getOpenDisabledReason(stage)}
          className="rounded border border-gray-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 transition hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          Открыть
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PipelineStageRow;

import React from "react";
import type {
  StageMetaDTO,
  StageRepresentationDTO,
} from "../../../types";

interface Props {
  representation: StageRepresentationDTO;
  meta: StageMetaDTO | null;
}

function cx(...classes: Array<string | false | null | undefined>): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

export const StageRepresentationBanner: React.FC<Props> = ({
  representation,
  meta,
}) => {
  const isCanonical = representation.is_canonical;
  const previewSourceLabel = representation.provenance?.preview_source_label;
  const textEnriched = meta?.text_enriched === true;

  const modeLabel = isCanonical ? "Canonical" : "Friendly";
  const modeHint = isCanonical
    ? "Каноническое представление стадии"
    : "Дружественное представление стадии";

  const modeToneClasses = isCanonical
    ? "border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"
    : "border-blue-300 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700";

  const localizedRepresentationLabel =
    representation.label ||
    (isCanonical ? "Каноническое представление" : "Дружественное представление");

  const localizedDescription =
    representation.description ||
    (isCanonical
      ? "Отображаются канонические данные стадии без дополнительного пользовательского обогащения."
      : "Отображаются данные стадии в пользовательском режиме просмотра.");

  const localizedPreviewSource =
    previewSourceLabel === "CHUNKS"
      ? "Источник preview: CHUNKS"
      : previewSourceLabel
        ? `Источник preview: ${previewSourceLabel}`
        : null;

  const localizedEnrichment = textEnriched
    ? "Текст обогащён из CHUNKS"
    : null;

  const shouldRender =
    Boolean(localizedRepresentationLabel) ||
    Boolean(localizedDescription) ||
    Boolean(localizedPreviewSource) ||
    Boolean(localizedEnrichment);

  if (!shouldRender) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="border-b bg-gray-50 px-5 py-2">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-gray-600">
        <span
          className={cx(
            "inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 font-medium",
            modeToneClasses
          )}
          title={modeHint}
        >
          {modeLabel}
        </span>

        <span className="font-medium text-gray-800">
          {localizedRepresentationLabel}
        </span>

        {localizedPreviewSource && (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-amber-700">
            {localizedPreviewSource}
          </span>
        )}

        {localizedEnrichment && (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-emerald-700">
            {localizedEnrichment}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {localizedDescription && (
        <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-4 text-gray-500">
          {localizedDescription}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default StageRepresentationBanner;

import React from "react";

  status: PipelineStageStatus;

const STATUS_CONFIG: Record<
  PipelineStageStatus,
  { label: string; title: string; className: string }
> = {

    label: "Готово",

    >
      {cfg.label}
    </span>

>>> FILE_START | index=43 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/documents/pipeline/StructureStageViewer.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import type {
  PipelineStageViewDTO,
  StructurePreviewDTO,
  StructureSectionPreviewDTO,
} from "../../../types";
import { ChunksStageViewer } from "./ChunksStageViewer";

interface Props {
  structureStage: PipelineStageViewDTO;
  chunksStage: PipelineStageViewDTO | null;
}

interface SegmentSectionGroup {
  segmentId: string;
  label: string;
  segmentType: string | null;
  isPrimary: boolean;
  order: number | null;
  sections: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[];
}

function cx(...classes: Array<string | false | null | undefined>): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

function isStructurePreview(
  preview: PipelineStageViewDTO["preview"],
): preview is StructurePreviewDTO {
  return Boolean(preview && (preview as StructurePreviewDTO).type === "structure");
}

function isCanonicalStructureView(stage: PipelineStageViewDTO): boolean {
  return stage.representation?.is_canonical ?? true;
}

function formatPageRange(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string | null {
  const from =
    typeof section.page_from === "number"
      ? section.page_from
      : section.page_from ?? null;
  const to =
    typeof section.page_to === "number"
      ? section.page_to
      : section.page_to ?? null;

  if (from == null && to == null) return null;
  if (from != null && to != null) {
    return from === to ? `стр. ${from}` : `стр. ${from}–${to}`;
  }
  return `стр. ${from ?? to ?? "?"}`;
}

function hasChunksMapping(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): boolean {
  return (
    section.source_mapping?.source_stage === "CHUNKS" &&
    (section.source_mapping?.chunk_indices?.length ?? 0) > 0
  );
}

function getSectionJson(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(section, null, 2);
  } catch {
    return JSON.stringify({ id: section.id }, null, 2);
  }
}

/**
 * Canonical text секции:
 * - используется только structure_text;
 * - UI не использует дополнительные текстовые поля.
 */
function getSectionTextCanonical(
  section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO,
): {
  text: string;
  hasText: boolean;
} {
  const structure = (section.structure_text ?? "").trim();

  const text = structure;

  return {
    text,
    hasText: text.length > 0,
  };
}

function getSectionTextFriendly(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string {
  const enriched = (section.enriched_text ?? "").trim();
  const structure = (section.structure_text ?? "").trim();

  return enriched || structure;
}

function getSectionLabel(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string {
  return (section.title ?? "").trim() || "Без названия";
}

function getSectionMetaLine(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];

  parts.push(`Уровень ${section.level ?? 1}`);

  const pages = formatPageRange(section);
  if (pages) {
    parts.push(pages);
  }

  if (section.clause_uid) {
    parts.push(`clause_uid: ${section.clause_uid}`);
  }

  if (section.segment_type) {
    parts.push(
      `segment: ${section.segment_type}${
        section.is_primary_segment ? " (PRIMARY)" : ""
      }`,
    );
  }

  return parts.join(" · ");
}

/**
 * Короткое описание для навигационного списка слева.
 * Используется только v2-логика: enriched -> structure.
 */
function getSectionNavDescription(section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO): string {
  const enriched = (section.enriched_text ?? "").trim();
  const structure = (section.structure_text ?? "").trim();

  const text = enriched || structure;
  if (!text) return "";

  return text.length > 200 ? `${text.slice(0, 200)}…` : text;
}

function groupSectionsBySegment(
  preview: StructurePreviewDTO,
): SegmentSectionGroup[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(preview.segments) || preview.segments.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  const result: SegmentSectionGroup[] = [];

  for (const seg of preview.segments) {
    if (
      !seg ||
      !seg.segment ||
      !Array.isArray(seg.sections) ||
      seg.sections.length === 0
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const s = seg.segment;
    const segmentId = s.segment_id;
    const title = s.segment_title ?? "(без заголовка)";
    const type = s.segment_type ?? "primary";
    const order =
      typeof s.segment_order === "number" ? s.segment_order : null;
    const isPrimary = !!s.is_primary;

    const labelParts: string[] = [];
    if (order != null) labelParts.push(`#${order}`);
    labelParts.push(title);
    labelParts.push(type);
    if (isPrimary) labelParts.push("PRIMARY");

    result.push({
      segmentId,
      label: labelParts.join(" · "),
      segmentType: s.segment_type ?? null,
      isPrimary,
      order,
      sections: seg.sections,
    });
  }

  result.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.isPrimary && !b.isPrimary) return -1;
    if (!a.isPrimary && b.isPrimary) return 1;
    const ao = a.order ?? 0;
    const bo = b.order ?? 0;
    return ao - bo;
  });

  return result;
}

export const StructureStageViewer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  structureStage,
  chunksStage,
}) => {
  const status = structureStage.status;
  const preview = structureStage.preview;

  // V2_ONLY: сначала честно обрабатываем status,
  // и только потом смотрим на наличие preview.
  if (status === "pending") {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Структура документа ещё не готова в v2-формате. Попробуй позже.
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (status === "missing") {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
        Структурированный артефакт ожидается, но недоступен или повреждён.
        Проверь состояние пайплайна для стадии STRUCTURE.
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isStructurePreview(preview)) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Для этой стадии нет STRUCTURE-превью.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isCanonical = isCanonicalStructureView(structureStage);

  const segmentGroups = React.useMemo(
    () => groupSectionsBySegment(preview),
    [preview],
  );

  const hasSegmentGroups = segmentGroups.length > 0;

  const sections: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[] = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!hasSegmentGroups) {
      return [];
    }

    const all: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[] = [];
    for (const group of segmentGroups) {
      all.push(...group.sections);
    }
    return all;
  }, [hasSegmentGroups, segmentGroups]);

  const sectionCount = structureStage.meta?.section_count ?? sections.length;

  const [activeSectionId, setActiveSectionId] = React.useState<string | null>(
    sections[0]?.id ?? null,
  );

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setActiveSectionId((prev) => {
      if (prev && sections.some((s) => s.id === prev)) {
        return prev;
      }
      return sections[0]?.id ?? null;
    });
  }, [sections]);

  const activeSection: StructureSectionPreviewDTO | null = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!sections.length) return null;
    if (!activeSectionId) return sections[0];
    return sections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) ?? sections[0];
  }, [sections, activeSectionId]);

  const highlightedChunkIndices =
    activeSection && activeSection.source_mapping?.source_stage === "CHUNKS"
      ? activeSection.source_mapping.chunk_indices ?? []
      : [];

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[25%_30%_45%]">
      <aside className="flex min-h-0 flex-col rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white">
        <div className="border-b border-gray-100 px-3 py-2">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
            Структура документа
          </p>
          {sectionCount > 0 && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
              Секций: {sectionCount}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto px-1 py-2">
          {sections.length > 0 ? (
            hasSegmentGroups ? (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {segmentGroups.map((group) => (
                  <div key={group.segmentId}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-2 pb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                      <span className="truncate">{group.label}</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      {group.sections.map((section) => (
                        <SectionNavItem
                          key={section.id}
                          section={section}
                          isActive={section.id === activeSectionId}
                          onSelect={() => setActiveSectionId(section.id)}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {sections.map((section) => (
                  <SectionNavItem
                    key={section.id}
                    section={section}
                    isActive={section.id === activeSectionId}
                    onSelect={() => setActiveSectionId(section.id)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )
          ) : (
            <div className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-500">
              В STRUCTURE пока нет секций.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section className="min-h-0 min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
        {isCanonical ? (
          <CanonicalSectionPanel
            sections={sections}
            activeSectionId={activeSectionId}
          />
        ) : (
          <FriendlySectionPanel
            sections={sections}
            activeSectionId={activeSectionId}
          />
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="min-h-0 min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3">
        {isCanonical ? (
          <CanonicalJsonPanel
            sections={sections}
            activeSectionId={activeSectionId}
            sourceLabel="Здесь показывается текущий объект секции из preview.segments[].sections[]."
          />
        ) : chunksStage ? (
          chunksStage.status === "ready" ? (
            <ChunksStageViewer
              stage={chunksStage}
              highlightedChunkIndices={highlightedChunkIndices}
              traceLabel={
                highlightedChunkIndices.length > 0
                  ? "Фрагменты исходного текста, привязанные к выбранной секции"
                  : "Для выбранной секции не найдено связанных чанков"
              }
            />
          ) : chunksStage.status === "pending" ? (
            <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
              CHUNKS ещё не готовы в v2-формате. Попробуй позже.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-red-700">
              CHUNKS-артефакт ожидается, но недоступен или повреждён.
            </div>
          )
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
            CHUNKS-стадия недоступна — показать исходный текст невозможно.
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

const SectionNavItem: React.FC<{
  section: StructureSectionPreviewDTO;
  isActive: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
}> = ({ section, isActive, onSelect }) => {
  const indent = Math.max(0, (section.level ?? 1) - 1);
  const hasClauseUid = Boolean(section.clause_uid);
  const hasSourceChunks = hasChunksMapping(section);
  const pageRange = formatPageRange(section);
  const title = getSectionLabel(section);
  const preview = getSectionNavDescription(section);

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onSelect}
      className={cx(
        "flex w-full items-start gap-2 rounded-md py-1.5 text-left text-xs transition-colors",
        isActive ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-800" : "text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50",
      )}
      style={{ paddingLeft: `${8 + indent * 12}px`, paddingRight: "8px" }}
      title={[
        section.clause_uid ? `clause_uid: ${section.clause_uid}` : null,
        pageRange,
        section.segment_type
          ? `segment: ${section.segment_type}${
              section.is_primary_segment ? " (PRIMARY)" : ""
            }`
          : null,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ")}
    >
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="truncate">{title}</div>
        {preview ? (
          <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[11px] text-gray-400">
            {preview}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {hasSourceChunks ? (
        <span
          className="mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"
          aria-label="Есть привязка к CHUNKS"
        />
      ) : hasClauseUid ? (
        <span
          className="mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-blue-400"
          aria-label="Есть clause_uid"
        />
      ) : null}
    </button>
  );
};

const CanonicalSectionPanel: React.FC<{
  sections: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[];
  activeSectionId: string | null;
}> = ({ sections, activeSectionId }) => {
  if (!sections.length) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        В STRUCTURE пока нет секций.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const section =
    activeSectionId != null
      ? sections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) ?? sections[0]
      : sections[0];

  if (!section) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        Выбери секцию слева.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const pageRange = formatPageRange(section);
  const sourceMapped = hasChunksMapping(section);
  const { text: previewText, hasText } = getSectionTextCanonical(section);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <div className="mb-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          Сведения о секции
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
          В canonical‑режиме здесь показывается узел структуры документа
          (structure_text), а не enriched‑текст из CHUNKS.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">
        <div>
          <p
            className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900"
            title={section.clause_uid ? `clause_uid: ${section.clause_uid}` : undefined}
          >
            {getSectionLabel(section)}
          </p>

          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
            {getSectionMetaLine(section)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {sourceMapped ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-700">
              Есть привязка к CHUNKS
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-amber-700">
              Нет привязки к CHUNKS для этой секции
            </span>
          )}

          {section.segment_type ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-gray-700">
              {section.segment_type}
              {section.is_primary_segment ? " · PRIMARY" : ""}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
          <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
            Поля узла
          </p>

          <dl className="space-y-2 text-sm text-gray-700">
            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">ID</dt>
              <dd className="break-all">{section.id || "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Clause UID
              </dt>
              <dd>{section.clause_uid || "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Уровень
              </dt>
              <dd>{section.level ?? 1}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Страницы
              </dt>
              <dd>{pageRange ?? "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Segment ID
              </dt>
              <dd>{section.segment_id || "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Segment Type
              </dt>
              <dd>{section.segment_type || "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Segment Title
              </dt>
              <dd>{section.segment_title || "—"}</dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Segment Order
              </dt>
              <dd>
                {typeof section.segment_order === "number"
                  ? section.segment_order
                  : "—"}
              </dd>
            </div>

            <div>
              <dt className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
                Primary Segment
              </dt>
              <dd>
                {typeof section.is_primary_segment === "boolean"
                  ? section.is_primary_segment
                    ? "true"
                    : "false"
                  : "—"}
              </dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        </div>

        {hasText ? (
          <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
              Текст clause tree (structure_text)
            </p>
            <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">
              {previewText}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="rounded-md border border-dashed border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              Для этого узла текст clause tree не задан. Это нормально для canonical
              STRUCTURE, если в дереве есть только заголовок и метаданные узла.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const FriendlySectionPanel: React.FC<{
  sections: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[];
  activeSectionId: string | null;
}> = ({ sections, activeSectionId }) => {
  if (!sections.length) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        В STRUCTURE пока нет секций.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const section =
    activeSectionId != null
      ? sections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) ?? sections[0]
      : sections[0];

  if (!section) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        Выбери секцию слева.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const sourceMapped = hasChunksMapping(section);
  const previewText = getSectionTextFriendly(section);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <div className="mb-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          Текст секции
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
          В friendly‑режиме здесь показывается текст секции, обогащённый из CHUNKS
          (enriched_text), с fallback на structure_text.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-4">
        <div>
          <p
            className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900"
            title={section.clause_uid ? `clause_uid: ${section.clause_uid}` : undefined}
          >
            {getSectionLabel(section)}
          </p>

          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-gray-400">
            {getSectionMetaLine(section)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {sourceMapped ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-700">
              Есть привязка к CHUNKS
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-amber-700">
              Нет привязки к CHUNKS для этой секции
            </span>
          )}

          {section.segment_type ? (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-gray-700">
              {section.segment_type}
              {section.is_primary_segment ? " · PRIMARY" : ""}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        {previewText ? (
          <div className="rounded-md bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
            <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm leading-6 text-gray-700">
              {previewText}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="rounded-md border border-dashed border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-3 py-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              Для этой секции нет текстового превью.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const CanonicalJsonPanel: React.FC<{
  sections: StructureSectionPreviewDTO[];
  activeSectionId: string | null;
  sourceLabel: string;
}> = ({ sections, activeSectionId, sourceLabel }) => {
  if (!sections.length) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        В STRUCTURE пока нет секций.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const section =
    activeSectionId != null
      ? sections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) ?? sections[0]
      : sections[0];

  if (!section) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-gray-500">
        Выбери секцию слева.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="mb-3 border-b border-gray-100 pb-2">
        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500">
          JSON узла
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">{sourceLabel}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-gray-950 p-3">
        <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs leading-5 text-gray-100">
          {getSectionJson(section)}
        </pre>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default StructureStageViewer;

// =============================================================
// src/components/evidence/EvidencePanel.tsx
// Полноценная панель доказательств для одного результата проверки.
// Правый сайдбар: контекст документа, фрагмент, QC, флаги качества,
// классификация документа, условная проверка, извлечённые данные.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";

import type {
  CheckResult,
  QCStatus,
  OpenViewerAtFn,
  StructuredField,
} from "../../types";
import { SeverityBadge } from "../ui/SeverityBadge";
import { ClassificationBadge } from "../ui/ClassificationBadge";
import { useEvidence } from "../../api/evidence";
import type { EvidenceDetail } from "../../api/evidence";


interface EvidencePanelProps {
  result: CheckResult;
  onClose: () => void;
  onQcChange?: (
    result: CheckResult,
    newStatus: Exclude<QCStatus, "pending">,
  ) => void;
  onOpenAtLocation?: OpenViewerAtFn;
}

export const EvidencePanel: React.FC<EvidencePanelProps> = ({
  result,
  onClose,
  onQcChange,
  onOpenAtLocation,
}) => {
  const { data: evidence, isLoading, error } = useEvidence(result.id);

  // evidence уже один объект, а не массив
  const evidenceObj: EvidenceDetail | undefined = evidence ?? undefined;

  const snippet =
    evidenceObj?.quote ||
    evidenceObj?.snippet ||
    result.value_text ||
    "(нет фрагмента текста)";

  const page = evidenceObj?.page;
  const clauseUid = evidenceObj?.clause_uid ?? null;

  const evidenceDetails = evidenceObj?.structured_data ?? undefined;

  const hasEvidenceDetails =
    !!evidenceDetails &&
    typeof evidenceDetails === "object" &&
    Object.keys(evidenceDetails).length > 0;

  const qcStatus = result.qc_status;
  const isPendingQc = qcStatus === "pending";

  const handleApprove = () => {
    onQcChange?.(result, "approved");
  };

  const handleReject = () => {
    onQcChange?.(result, "rejected");
  };

  const handleViewOriginal = () => {
    if (!onOpenAtLocation) return;

    onOpenAtLocation({
      documentId: result.document_id,
      page: evidenceObj?.page ?? null,
      clauseUid: clauseUid ?? null,
      elementId: null,

      segmentId: evidenceObj?.segment_id ?? null,
      chunkId: evidenceObj?.chunk_id ?? null,
      chunkIndex: evidenceObj?.chunk_index ?? null,
    });
  };

  const qualitySummary = React.useMemo(() => {
    // quality_flags: Record<string, boolean> | undefined
    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = result.quality_flags ?? {};
    const active: string[] = [];

    if (flags.structure_degraded) {
      active.push("Структура документа нарушена");
    }
    if (flags.llm_fallback_used) {
      active.push("Использован LLM fallback");
    }
    if (!active.length && Object.keys(flags).length) {
      active.push("Есть технические флаги (см. детали ниже)");
    }
    if (!active.length) {
      return "Флаги качества не установлены.";
    }
    return active.join(" · ");
  }, [result.quality_flags]);

  // Секция "Извлечённые данные": из structured_data CheckResult.
  const structuredEntries: [string, StructuredField][] = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (!result.structured_data || typeof result.structured_data !== "object") {
      return [];
    }
    return Object.entries(result.structured_data) as [
      string,
      StructuredField,
    ][];
  }, [result.structured_data]);

  const hasStructuredData = structuredEntries.length > 0;

  // Простейшая логика статуса по полю: found + confidence.
  const getFieldStatus = (
    field: StructuredField,
  ): "yes" | "no" | "unclear" => {
    const found = field.found ?? true;
    const conf = typeof field.confidence === "number" ? field.confidence : 0;
    if (!found) return "no";
    if (conf >= 0.6) return "yes";
    return "unclear";
  };

  const renderStatusLabel = (field: StructuredField) => {
    const status = getFieldStatus(field);
    if (status === "yes") return "Есть";
    if (status === "no") return "Нет";
    return "Требует уточнения";
  };

  // Человекочитаемый текст для location.
  const renderLocationLabel = (field: StructuredField) => {
    if (!field.location) return "—";
    const { page: locPage, clause_uid } = field.location;
    if (!locPage && !clause_uid) return "Фрагмент";
    if (locPage && clause_uid) return `Стр. ${locPage}, п. ${clause_uid}`;
    if (locPage) return `Стр. ${locPage}`;
    return clause_uid || "Фрагмент";
  };

  // Краткий QC-статус по полю. Пока только текст, без кнопок.
  const renderQcLabel = (field: StructuredField) => {
    const status = field.qc?.status || "pending";
    if (status === "pending") return "pending";
    if (status === "accepted") return "accepted";
    if (status === "rejected") return "rejected";
    if (status === "auto_accepted") return "auto_accepted";
    return status;
  };

  // Открытие viewer на конкретном поле.
  const handleOpenFieldLocation = (field: StructuredField) => {
    if (!field.location || !onOpenAtLocation) return;
    onOpenAtLocation({
      documentId: result.document_id,
      page: field.location.page ?? field.location.start_page ?? null,
      clauseUid: field.location.clause_uid ?? null,
      elementId: field.location.element_id ?? null,

      segmentId: field.location.segment_id ?? null,
      chunkId: field.location.chunk_id ?? null,
      chunkIndex: field.location.chunk_index ?? null,
    });
  };

  // Низкая уверенность классификации
  const isLowConfidence =
    typeof result.doc_classification_confidence === "number" &&
    result.doc_classification_confidence < 0.6;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-y-0 right-0 z-40 w-full max-w-3xl bg-white shadow-xl border-l flex flex-col transform transition-transform duration-200 ease-out translate-x-0">
      {/* Шапка */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-3 border-b">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400">Документ</div>
          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-900">
            {result.document_name || "Без имени"}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500">
            {result.doc_type && <span>Тип: {result.doc_type}</span>}
            <button
              type="button"
              className="text-blue-600 hover:underline px-0 py-0"
              onClick={handleViewOriginal}
            >
              Просмотреть исходный документ
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <SeverityBadge severity={result.severity} />
              {result.conditional_check_triggered && (
                <span
                  className="text-[13px]"
                  title={result.trigger_name || "Conditional check triggered"}
                >
                  ⚡
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
              Результат: {result.result}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
              QC: {qcStatus}
              {result.qc_by && ` · ${result.qc_by}`}
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-lg"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Закрыть"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Основное содержимое */}
      <div className="flex-1 grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 px-4 py-3 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Левая колонка: контекст */}
        <div className="col-span-1 border-r pr-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-4">
          {/* Документ */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Документ
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-800">
              {result.document_name || "Без имени"}
            </div>
            {result.doc_type && (
              <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
                Тип: {result.doc_type}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Классификация документа */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Классификация документа
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-gray-700">
              {result.doc_classification_level ? (
                <ClassificationBadge
                  level={result.doc_classification_level}
                  confidence={result.doc_classification_confidence}
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                  Нет данных классификации
                </span>
              )}
              {isLowConfidence && (
                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-yellow-50 text-[11px] text-yellow-700 border border-yellow-200">
                  Низкая уверенность
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Условная проверка */}
          {result.conditional_check_triggered && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
                Условная проверка
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200 text-[11px]">
                  ⚡ {result.trigger_name || "Условная проверка"}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                  Эта проверка запущена условным правилом
                  {result.trigger_name ? ` «${result.trigger_name}»` : ""}.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Проверка */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Проверка
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-800">{result.check_name}</div>
            {result.detector_used && (
              <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
                Источник: {result.detector_used}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Метаданные */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Метаданные
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 space-y-0.5">
              {result.confidence != null && (
                <div>
                  Уверенность детектора:{" "}
                  {Math.round(result.confidence * 100)}%
                </div>
              )}
              <div>
                Создано:{" "}
                {result.created_at
                  ? new Date(result.created_at).toLocaleString()
                  : "—"}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Средняя колонка: фрагмент + структурированные данные */}
        <div className="col-span-1 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden">
          {/* Цитата / фрагмент */}
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Цитата / фрагмент
            </div>
            {isLoading && (
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 py-2">
                Загружаем evidence…
              </div>
            )}
            {error && (
              <div className="text-xs text-red-600 py-2">
                Не удалось загрузить evidence.
              </div>
            )}
            {!isLoading && !error && (
              <>
                {(page || clauseUid) && (
                  <div className="mb-1 text-[11px] text-gray-500 flex gap-3">
                    {page && <span>Стр. {page}</span>}
                    {clauseUid && <span>Clause: {clauseUid}</span>}
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="border rounded bg-gray-50 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed max-h-48 overflow-auto">
                  {snippet}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Извлечённые данные (structured_data) */}
          {hasStructuredData && (
            <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
                Извлечённые данные
              </div>
              <div className="border rounded overflow-auto max-h-48">
                <table className="w-full text-[11px]">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b">
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Поле
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Значение
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Статус
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Источник
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Увер.
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        Фрагмент
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-2 py-1 text-left text-gray-500">
                        QC
                      </th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {structuredEntries.map(([key, field]) => {
                      const value =
                        field.value != null ? String(field.value) : "—";
                      const source = field.source || "—";
                      const conf =
                        typeof field.confidence === "number"
                          ? `${Math.round(field.confidence * 100)}%`
                          : "—";
                      const hasLocation = !!field.location;

                      return (
                        <tr key={key} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 whitespace-nowrap">
                            {key}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-800">
                            {value}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                            {renderStatusLabel(field)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                            {source}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                            {conf}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top whitespace-nowrap">
                            {hasLocation ? (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="text-blue-600 hover:underline"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  handleOpenFieldLocation(field)
                                }
                              >
                                {renderLocationLabel(field)}
                              </button>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-gray-400">—</span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-2 py-1 align-top text-gray-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                            {renderQcLabel(field)}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Детали из evidence.structured_data (технические) */}
          {hasEvidenceDetails && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
                Детали проверки
              </div>
              <div className="border rounded bg-white p-2 text-[11px]">
                {result.qc_comment && (
                  <>
                    <div className="mb-1 text-gray-500">
                      Комментарий QC:
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap mb-2">
                      {result.qc_comment}
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
                <details>
                  <summary className="cursor-pointer text-blue-600">
                    Показать тех. детали
                  </summary>
                  <pre className="mt-1 text-[10px] bg-gray-50 rounded p-2 overflow-auto">
                    {JSON.stringify(evidenceDetails, null, 2)}
                  </pre>
                </details>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Правая колонка: QC и флаги качества */}
        <div className="col-span-1 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden">
          {/* QC-статус */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              QC-статус
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-800 mb-1">
              {qcStatus}
              {result.qc_by && ` · ${result.qc_by}`}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2">
              {isPendingQc
                ? "Требуется QC-решение по этому результату."
                : "QC по этому результату уже выставлен."}
            </div>
            {onQcChange && (
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-green-700 border-green-300 hover:bg-green-50 disabled:opacity-40"
                  onClick={handleApprove}
                  disabled={!isPendingQc}
                >
                  ✅ Согласен
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-red-700 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-40"
                  onClick={handleReject}
                  disabled={!isPendingQc}
                >
                  ❌ Не согласен
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Флаги качества */}
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400 mb-1">
              Флаги качества
            </div>
            <div className="border rounded bg-white p-2 text-[11px] max-h-64 overflow-auto">
              <div className="mb-2 text-gray-700">{qualitySummary}</div>
              {result.quality_flags &&
                Object.keys(result.quality_flags).length > 0 && (
                  <details>
                    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-blue-600">
                      Показать тех. детали
                    </summary>
                    <pre className="mt-1 text-[10px] bg-gray-50 rounded p-2 overflow-auto">
                      {JSON.stringify(result.quality_flags, null, 2)}
                    </pre>
                  </details>
                )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default EvidencePanel;

// =============================================================
// src/components/evidence/MagicZoom.tsx
// Magic Zoom: быстрый просмотр результата проверки для юриста.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

import type { CheckResult, QCStatus } from "../../types";
import { SeverityBadge } from "../ui/SeverityBadge";
import { ClassificationBadge } from "../ui/ClassificationBadge";
import { useEvidence } from "../../api/evidence";

interface MagicZoomProps {
  result: CheckResult;
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenFull?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onQcChange?: (
    result: CheckResult,
    newStatus: Exclude<QCStatus, "pending">,
  ) => void;
}

type EvidenceDto = {
  snippet?: string;
  quote?: string;
  page?: number;
  clause_uid?: string;
};

export const MagicZoom: React.FC<MagicZoomProps> = ({
  result,
  onClose,
  onOpenFull,
  onQcChange,
}) => {
  const { data: evidence, isLoading, error } = useEvidence(result.id);

  // SSR‑safe модальный корень
  const modalRoot =
    typeof document !== "undefined" ? document.body : null;

  const handleBackdropClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onClose();
  };

  const handleContentClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
  };

  const handleOpenFull = () => {
    onOpenFull?.(result);
  };

  const handleApprove = () => {
    onQcChange?.(result, "approved");
  };

  const handleReject = () => {
    onQcChange?.(result, "rejected");
  };

  const evidenceObj = (evidence ?? {}) as EvidenceDto;

  const snippet =
    evidenceObj.snippet ||
    evidenceObj.quote ||
    result.value_text ||
    "(нет фрагмента текста)";

  const page = evidenceObj.page;
  const clauseUid = evidenceObj.clause_uid;

  const triggered = result.conditional_check_triggered;
  const triggerName = result.trigger_name;
  const detector = result.detector_used;
  const qcStatus = result.qc_status;

  if (!modalRoot) {
    // На случай SSR — просто ничего не рендерим
    return null;
  }

  return createPortal(
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-start justify-center bg-black/40"
      onClick={handleBackdropClick}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div
        className="mt-20 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-lg bg-white shadow-lg border p-4 text-sm"
        onClick={handleContentClick}
      >
        {/* Шапка: документ + риск + QC */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400">
              Документ
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-900">
              {result.document_name || "Без имени"}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500">
              {result.doc_type && <span>Тип: {result.doc_type}</span>}
              {triggered && (
                <span
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200"
                  title={
                    triggerName
                      ? `Условная проверка по триггеру: ${triggerName}`
                      : "Условная проверка (conditional)"
                  }
                >
                  ⚡ {triggerName || "условная"}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <SeverityBadge severity={result.severity} />
                {/* Бейдж классификации документа (денормализовано в CheckResult) */}
                {result.doc_classification_level && (
                  <ClassificationBadge
                    level={result.doc_classification_level}
                    confidence={result.doc_classification_confidence}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                Результат: {result.result}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">
                QC: {qcStatus}
                {result.qc_by && ` · ${result.qc_by}`}
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-lg"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Закрыть"
            >
              ×
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Информация о проверке */}
        <div className="mb-2">
          <div className="text-[11px] uppercase text-gray-400">Проверка</div>
          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-900">
            {result.check_name}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
            {detector && <>Источник: {detector}</>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Состояние загрузки evidence */}
        {isLoading && (
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 py-2">
            Загружаем фрагмент документа…
          </div>
        )}
        {error && (
          <div className="text-xs text-red-600 py-2">
            Не удалось загрузить evidence.
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Метаданные фрагмента */}
        {!isLoading && !error && (page || clauseUid) && (
          <div className="mb-1 text-[11px] text-gray-500 flex gap-3">
            {page && <span>Стр. {page}</span>}
            {clauseUid && <span>Clause: {clauseUid}</span>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Фрагмент / цитата */}
        {!isLoading && !error && (
          <div className="mt-1 border rounded bg-gray-50 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed max-h-64 overflow-auto">
            {snippet}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Нижняя панель действий */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
            {result.confidence != null && (
              <>Уверенность: {Math.round(result.confidence * 100)}% · </>
            )}
            Создано:{" "}
            {result.created_at
              ? new Date(result.created_at).toLocaleString()
              : "—"}
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {onQcChange && (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-green-700 border-green-300 hover:bg-green-50"
                  onClick={handleApprove}
                >
                  ✅ Согласен
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-red-700 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50"
                  onClick={handleReject}
                >
                  ❌ Не согласен
                </button>
              </>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
              onClick={onClose}
            >
              Закрыть
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="px-3 py-1 rounded bg-blue-600 text-white text-xs hover:bg-blue-700"
              onClick={handleOpenFull}
            >
              Открыть полностью
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    modalRoot,
  );
};

export default MagicZoom;
