// =============================================================
// src/components/grid/DDGrid.tsx
// DD Grid:
// - режим 1: «плоский» список CheckResult (AG Grid);
// - режим 2: матрица документ × риск (wide-format, AG Grid).
// =============================================================

import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { AgGridReact } from "ag-grid-react";
import type {
  ColDef,
  ICellRendererParams,
  ValueGetterParams,
  SelectionChangedEvent,
  ITooltipParams,
} from "ag-grid-community";

import type {
  CheckResult,
  OpenViewerAtFn,
  StructuredField,
  ClassificationLevel,
  SeverityLevel,
} from "../../types";
import { SeverityBadge } from "../ui/SeverityBadge";
import { ClassificationBadge } from "../ui/ClassificationBadge";
import QCCellRenderer from "../qc/QCCellRenderer";
import QualityFlagRenderer from "../qc/QualityFlagRenderer";

// AG Grid стили
import "ag-grid-community/styles/ag-grid.css";
import "ag-grid-community/styles/ag-theme-alpine.css";

interface DDGridProps {
  results: CheckResult[];
  onEyeClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onDocumentClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onCheckClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onQcChange?: (
    result: CheckResult,
    newStatus: "approved" | "rejected",
  ) => void;

  // для bulk QC:
  // - в режиме list: ids результатов (CheckResult.id)
  // - в режиме matrix: ids документов (document_id)
  onSelectionChange?: (ids: string[]) => void;

  // Режим отображения: матрица или список
  mode?: "matrix" | "list";

  // Открыть viewer на конкретном фрагменте
  onOpenAtLocation?: OpenViewerAtFn;
}

interface GridContext {
  onEyeClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onDocumentClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onCheckClick?: (result: CheckResult) => void;
  onQcChange?: (
    result: CheckResult,
    newStatus: "approved" | "rejected",
  ) => void;
  onOpenAtLocation?: OpenViewerAtFn;
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Типы для матричного режима (строка = документ)
// -------------------------------------------------------------

interface MatrixRowBase {
  documentId: string;
  documentName: string;
  docType?: string | null;
  classificationLevel?: ClassificationLevel | null;
  classificationConfidence?: number | null;
  requiresManualReview?: boolean; // NEW: флаг manual review из DD Matrix

  totalCritical: number;
  totalHigh: number;
  totalMedium: number;
  totalUnclear: number;
}

// динамические поля: risk_<checkId> -> CheckResult
type MatrixRow = MatrixRowBase & {
  [key: `risk_${string}`]: CheckResult | undefined;
};

interface MatrixColumnMeta {
  checkId: string;
  checkName: string;
  origin?: string | null;
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Главный компонент
// -------------------------------------------------------------

export const DDGrid: React.FC<DDGridProps> = ({ mode = "matrix", ...rest }) => {
  if (mode === "list") {
    return <ListGrid {...rest} />;
  }
  return <MatrixGrid {...rest} />;
};

export default DDGrid;

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Матричный grid: документ × риск (AG Grid)
// -------------------------------------------------------------

const MatrixGrid: React.FC<DDGridProps> = ({
  results,
  onEyeClick,
  onDocumentClick,
  onCheckClick,
  onSelectionChange,
  onOpenAtLocation, // пока не используем в матрице, но оставляем в пропсах
}) => {
  const { rowData, riskColumns } = useMemo(() => {
    const docMap = new Map<string, MatrixRow>();
    const riskMetaMap = new Map<string, MatrixColumnMeta>();

    for (const r of results) {
      const docId = r.document_id;
      const checkId = r.check_id;

      if (!docId || !checkId) continue;

      let row = docMap.get(docId);
      if (!row) {
        row = {
          documentId: docId,
          documentName: r.document_name || "[no name]",
          docType: r.doc_type ?? null,
          classificationLevel: r.doc_classification_level ?? null,
          classificationConfidence: r.doc_classification_confidence ?? null,
          // Для матричного режима CheckResult приходит из DD Matrix:
          // backend кладёт туда метаданные документа (в т. ч. флаг manual review).
          requiresManualReview: r.doc_requires_manual_review ?? false,
          totalCritical: 0,
          totalHigh: 0,
          totalMedium: 0,
          totalUnclear: 0,
        };
        docMap.set(docId, row);
      }

      if (!riskMetaMap.has(checkId)) {
        riskMetaMap.set(checkId, {
          checkId,
          checkName: r.check_name || checkId,
          origin: r.origin ?? null,
        });
      }

      const colKey = `risk_${checkId}` as const;
      row[colKey] = r;

      if (r.severity === "critical") row.totalCritical += 1;
      if (r.severity === "high") row.totalHigh += 1;
      if (r.severity === "medium") row.totalMedium += 1;
      if (r.result === "unclear") row.totalUnclear += 1;
    }

    return {
      rowData: Array.from(docMap.values()),
      riskColumns: Array.from(riskMetaMap.values()),
    };
  }, [results]);

  const context: GridContext = {
    onEyeClick,
    onDocumentClick,
    onCheckClick,
    onQcChange: undefined,
    onOpenAtLocation,
  };

  const baseColumns = useMemo<ColDef<MatrixRow>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        headerName: "#",
        width: 60,
        valueGetter: (params) =>
          params.node?.rowIndex != null ? params.node.rowIndex + 1 : "",
        pinned: "left",
      },
      {
        headerName: "Документ",
        field: "documentName",
        minWidth: 220,
        flex: 2,
        pinned: "left",
        cellRenderer: (p: ICellRendererParams<MatrixRow>) => {
          const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            if (!onDocumentClick) return;
            const docId = p.data?.documentId;
            if (!docId) return;
            const first = results.find((r) => r.document_id === docId);
            if (first) onDocumentClick(first);
          };
          return (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleClick}
              className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline"
            >
              {p.value}
            </button>
          );
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "Тип документа",
        field: "docType",
        width: 140,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Классификация",
        field: "classificationLevel",
        width: 140,
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<MatrixRow>) => {
          const level = params.data?.classificationLevel;
          const conf = params.data?.classificationConfidence;
          if (!level) {
            return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
          }
          return (
            <ClassificationBadge
              level={level}
              confidence={conf ?? undefined}
              showConfidence={false}
            />
          );
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "Нужна проверка",
        field: "requiresManualReview",
        width: 140,
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<MatrixRow>) => {
          const v = params.data?.requiresManualReview;
          if (!v) {
            return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
          }
          return (
            <span
              className="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-yellow-50 text-[11px] text-yellow-700 border border-yellow-200"
              title="Классификатор пометил документ как требующий ручной проверки"
            >
              Нужна проверка
            </span>
          );
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "Crit",
        field: "totalCritical",
        width: 70,
      },
      {
        headerName: "High",
        field: "totalHigh",
        width: 70,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Med",
        field: "totalMedium",
        width: 70,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Unclear",
        field: "totalUnclear",
        width: 80,
      },
    ],
    [onDocumentClick, results],
  );

  const riskCols = useMemo<ColDef<MatrixRow>[]>(
    () =>
      riskColumns.map((meta) => {
        const field = `risk_${meta.checkId}` as const;

        return {
          headerName: meta.checkName,
          field,
          minWidth: 180,
          flex: 1,
          valueFormatter: (params) => {
            const res = params.data?.[field];
            if (!res) return "";
            if (res.result === "fail") {
              return res.severity ? `fail (${res.severity})` : "fail";
            }
            return res.result;
          },
          tooltipValueGetter: (
            params: ITooltipParams<MatrixRow>,
          ): string => {
            const res = params.data?.[field];
            return res?.value_text || "";
          },
          cellRenderer: (p: ICellRendererParams<MatrixRow>) => {
            const res = p.data?.[field];
            if (!res) {
              return (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>
              );
            }

            const { result, severity } = res;

            let textStatus = "";
            if (result === "fail") {
              if (severity === "critical" || severity === "high") {
                textStatus = "Требует внимания юриста";
              } else {
                textStatus = "Идентифицирован";
              }
            } else if (result === "unclear") {
              textStatus = "Требует внимания юриста";
            } else {
              textStatus = "Не найден";
            }

            const handleClick = (
              e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>,
            ) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              onEyeClick?.(res);
            };

            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClick}
                className="flex flex-col items-start gap-0.5 text-left w-full"
                title={res.value_text || ""}
              >
                <span className="text-[11px]">{textStatus}</span>
                {severity && (
                  <div className="scale-90 origin-left">
                    <SeverityBadge severity={severity} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          },
        } satisfies ColDef<MatrixRow>;
      }),
    [riskColumns, onEyeClick],
  );

  const columnDefs = useMemo<ColDef<MatrixRow>[]>(
    () => [...baseColumns, ...riskCols],
    [baseColumns, riskCols],
  );

  const defaultColDef = useMemo<ColDef>(
    () => ({
      sortable: true,
      filter: true,
      resizable: true,
    }),
    [],
  );

  const handleSelectionChanged = (
    event: SelectionChangedEvent<MatrixRow>,
  ) => {
    if (!onSelectionChange) return;
    const ids: string[] = event.api
      .getSelectedNodes()
      .map((n) => n.data!.documentId);
    onSelectionChange(ids);
  };

  if (!rowData.length) {
    return (
      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 p-3">
        Нет результатов проверок для отображения.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="ag-theme-alpine" style={{ height: 600, width: "100%" }}>
      <AgGridReact<MatrixRow>
        rowData={rowData}
        columnDefs={columnDefs}
        defaultColDef={defaultColDef}
        context={context}
        rowSelection={{
          mode: "multiRow",
          enableSelectionWithoutKeys: true,
          checkboxes: true,
          headerCheckbox: true,
        }}
        suppressCellFocus={true}
        onSelectionChanged={handleSelectionChanged}
        getRowId={(params) => params.data.documentId}
        theme="legacy"
      />
    </div>
  );
};

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Список (AG Grid) — для режима mode="list"
// -------------------------------------------------------------

const ListGrid: React.FC<DDGridProps> = ({
  results,
  onEyeClick,
  onDocumentClick,
  onCheckClick,
  onQcChange,
  onSelectionChange,
  onOpenAtLocation,
}) => {
  const context: GridContext = {
    onEyeClick,
    onDocumentClick,
    onCheckClick,
    onQcChange,
    onOpenAtLocation,
  };

  const columnDefs = useMemo<ColDef<CheckResult>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        headerName: "",
        width: 40,
        pinned: "left",
      },
      {
        headerName: "Risk",
        field: "severity",
        width: 170,
        pinned: "left",
        cellRenderer: SeverityCellRenderer,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Summary",
        field: "value_text",
        flex: 2,
        minWidth: 260,
        valueGetter: (params: ValueGetterParams<CheckResult>) =>
          params.data?.value_text || "",
      },
      {
        headerName: "Check",
        field: "check_name",
        flex: 1.8,
        minWidth: 220,
        cellRenderer: CheckNameCellRenderer,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Document",
        field: "document_name",
        flex: 2,
        minWidth: 220,
        cellRenderer: DocumentCellRenderer,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Doc Type",
        field: "doc_type",
        flex: 1,
        minWidth: 120,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Class.",
        field: "doc_classification_level",
        width: 130,
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<CheckResult>) => {
          const level = params.data?.doc_classification_level;
          const conf = params.data?.doc_classification_confidence;
          if (!level) {
            return (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>
            );
          }
          return (
            <ClassificationBadge
              level={level}
              confidence={conf}
            />
          );
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "Result",
        field: "result",
        flex: 0.8,
        minWidth: 110,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Conf.",
        field: "confidence",
        width: 90,
        valueFormatter: (params) => {
          const v = params.value as number | null | undefined;
          if (v == null) return "";
          return `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`;
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "QC",
        field: "qc_status",
        width: 160,
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<CheckResult>) => (
          <QCCellRenderer
            value={params.data?.qc_status ?? undefined}
            data={params.data as CheckResult}
            onQcChange={context.onQcChange}
          />
        ),
      },
      {
        headerName: "Flags",
        field: "quality_flags",
        width: 90,
        valueFormatter: (params) =>
          params.value ? JSON.stringify(params.value) : "",
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<CheckResult>) => (
          <QualityFlagRenderer result={params.data as CheckResult} />
        ),
      },
      {
        headerName: "Cond.",
        field: "conditional_check_triggered",
        width: 90,
        cellRenderer: ConditionalCellRenderer,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Trigger",
        field: "trigger_name",
        flex: 1,
        minWidth: 140,
        cellRenderer: (params: ICellRendererParams<CheckResult>) => {
          const triggered = params.data?.conditional_check_triggered;
          const name = params.data?.trigger_name;
          if (!triggered) {
            return (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>
            );
          }
          return (
            <span
              className="inline-flex items=center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200 text-[11px]"
              title={name || "Conditional check triggered"}
            >
              ⚡ {name || "Conditional"}
            </span>
          );
        },
      },
      {
        headerName: "Detector",
        field: "detector_used",
        flex: 0.8,
        minWidth: 110,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Extracted",
        field: "structured_data",
        flex: 1.4,
        minWidth: 220,
        cellRenderer: StructuredDataCellRenderer,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Origin",
        field: "origin",
        flex: 0.8,
        minWidth: 130,
      },
      {
        headerName: "Created",
        field: "created_at",
        minWidth: 160,
        hide: true,
        valueFormatter: (params) => {
          if (!params.value) return "";
          const d = new Date(params.value as string);
          return d.toLocaleString();
        },
      },
    ],
    [context.onQcChange],
  );

  const defaultColDef = useMemo<ColDef>(
    () => ({
      sortable: true,
      filter: true,
      resizable: true,
    }),
    [],
  );

  const handleSelectionChanged = (
    event: SelectionChangedEvent<CheckResult>,
  ) => {
    if (!onSelectionChange) return;
    const ids: string[] = event.api
      .getSelectedNodes()
      .map((n) => (n.data as CheckResult).id);
    onSelectionChange(ids);
  };

  return (
    <div className="ag-theme-alpine" style={{ height: 600, width: "100%" }}>
      <AgGridReact<CheckResult>
        rowData={results}
        columnDefs={columnDefs}
        defaultColDef={defaultColDef}
        context={context}
        rowSelection={{
          mode: "multiRow",
          enableSelectionWithoutKeys: true,
          checkboxes: true,
          headerCheckbox: true,
        }}
        suppressCellFocus={true}
        onSelectionChanged={handleSelectionChanged}
        getRowId={(params) => params.data.id}
        theme="legacy"
      />
    </div>
  );
};

// -------------------------------------------------------------
// Cell renderers для списка
// -------------------------------------------------------------

interface BaseCellParams
  extends ICellRendererParams<CheckResult, SeverityLevel | undefined> {
  context: GridContext;
  data: CheckResult;
}

const SeverityCellRenderer: React.FC<BaseCellParams> = (props) => {
  const { value, data, context } = props;

  const handleEyeClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    context.onEyeClick?.(data);
  };

  const showConditional = data.conditional_check_triggered;

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        {value ? (
          <SeverityBadge severity={value} />
        ) : (
          <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>
        )}
        {showConditional && (
          <span
            className="text-[13px]"
            title={data.trigger_name || "Conditional check triggered"}
          >
            ⚡
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="text-xs px-1 py-0.5 border rounded hover:bg-gray-100"
        onClick={handleEyeClick}
        title="Открыть подробности"
      >
        👁
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

const DocumentCellRenderer: React.FC<BaseCellParams> = (props) => {
  const { value, data, context } = props;

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    context.onDocumentClick?.(data);
  };

  if (!value) return <span className="text-gray-400">[no name]</span>;

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline"
      title="Показать все результаты по документу"
    >
      {value}
    </button>
  );
};

const CheckNameCellRenderer: React.FC<BaseCellParams> = (props) => {
  const { value, data, context } = props;

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    context.onCheckClick?.(data);
  };

  if (!value) return null;

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:underline"
      title="Показать все результаты этой проверки"
    >
      {value}
    </button>
  );
};

const ConditionalCellRenderer: React.FC<BaseCellParams> = (props) => {
  const { data } = props;
  const triggered = data.conditional_check_triggered;
  const triggerName = data.trigger_name;

  if (!triggered) {
    return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
  }

  return (
    <span
      className="text-[11px] text-purple-700"
      title={triggerName || "Conditional check triggered"}
    >
      ⚡
    </span>
  );
};

// Краткий renderer извлечённых данных для list‑таблицы.
// Показываем до 2 полей и даём клик "Фрагмент" при наличии location.
const StructuredDataCellRenderer: React.FC<BaseCellParams> = (props) => {
  const { data, context } = props;
  const sd = data.structured_data as
    | Record<string, StructuredField>
    | null
    | undefined;

  if (!sd || typeof sd !== "object") {
    return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
  }

  const entries = Object.entries(sd);
  if (!entries.length) {
    return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
  }

  const maxShown = 2;
  const shown = entries.slice(0, maxShown);
  const rest = entries.length - shown.length;

  const handleOpen = (field: StructuredField) => {
    if (!field.location || !context.onOpenAtLocation) return;
    context.onOpenAtLocation({
      documentId: data.document_id,
      page: field.location.page ?? field.location.start_page ?? null,
      clauseUid: field.location.clause_uid ?? null,
      elementId: field.location.element_id ?? null,
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 text-[11px]">
      {shown.map(([name, field]) => {
        const f = (field || {}) as StructuredField;
        const value = f.value != null ? String(f.value) : "—";
        const conf =
          typeof f.confidence === "number"
            ? `${Math.round(f.confidence * 100)}%`
            : "";
        const source = f.source || "";
        const hasLocation = !!f.location;

        return (
          <div key={name} className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <span className="text-gray-500 truncate max-w-[35%]">
              {name}
            </span>
            <span className="text-gray-800 truncate flex-1 text-right">
              {value}
              {conf && (
                <span className="text-gray-400 ml-1">({conf})</span>
              )}
              {source && (
                <span className="text-gray-400 ml-1">[{source}]</span>
              )}
            </span>
            {hasLocation && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="ml-1 text-blue-600 hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  handleOpen(f);
                }}
                title="Открыть фрагмент документа"
              >
                Фрагмент
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
      {rest > 0 && (
        <span className="text-gray-400">… ещё {rest}</span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// =============================================================
// src/components/grid/ExportButtons.tsx
// Кнопки экспорта для DD Grid.
// - Если есть выбранные строки -> экспортируем их.
// - Если нет выбранных -> экспортируем все результаты.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";
import type { CheckResult } from "../../types";
import {
  exportResultsToExcel,
  exportResultsToPdf,
  exportResultsToWord,
} from "../../utils/exportResults";

interface ExportButtonsProps {
  allResults: CheckResult[];
  selectedResults: CheckResult[];
  dealName?: string;
  runId?: string;

  /**
   * Дополнительный контекст запуска анализа для экспорта:
   *  - режим проверок (global_only / doc_type_only / global_and_doc_type)
   *  - стратегия пайплайна (REUSE_STRUCTURE / FULL_RERUN / ...)
   *
   * Эти поля не обязательны: если их нет, экспорт просто не будет
   * добавлять эту информацию в имя файла/шапку.
   */
  runChecksMode?:
    | "global_only"
    | "doc_type_only"
    | "global_and_doc_type";
  runPipelineStrategy?: string;
}

export const ExportButtons: React.FC<ExportButtonsProps> = ({
  allResults,
  selectedResults,
  dealName,
  runId,
  runChecksMode,
  runPipelineStrategy,
}) => {
  const hasSelection = selectedResults.length > 0;
  const baseResults = hasSelection ? selectedResults : allResults;

  const commonMeta = {
    dealName,
    runId,
    runChecksMode,
    runPipelineStrategy,
  };

  const handleExcel = () => {
    if (!baseResults.length) return;
    exportResultsToExcel(baseResults, commonMeta);
  };

  const handlePdf = () => {
    if (!baseResults.length) return;
    exportResultsToPdf(baseResults, commonMeta);
  };

  const handleWord = async () => {
    if (!baseResults.length) return;
    await exportResultsToWord(baseResults, commonMeta);
  };

  const labelSuffix = hasSelection
    ? `(${selectedResults.length} selected)`
    : "(all)";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-2">
      <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">
        Export {labelSuffix}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleExcel}
        className="px-2 py-1 text-xs border rounded hover:bg-gray-100"
      >
        Excel
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handlePdf}
        className="px-2 py-1 text-xs border rounded hover:bg-gray-100"
      >
        PDF
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleWord}
        className="px-2 py-1 text-xs border rounded hover:bg-gray-100"
      >
        Word
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ExportButtons;

================================================================================
// =============================================================
// src/components/grid/GridFilters.tsx
// Панель поиска и быстрых фильтров для DD Grid.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";

export type QuickFilter =
  | "all"
  | "critical"
  | "high"
  | "unclear"
  | "no_qc"
  | "important"
  | "conditional"
  | "low_confidence";

interface GridFiltersProps {
  searchText: string;
  onSearchChange: (text: string) => void;
  activeFilter: QuickFilter;
  onFilterChange: (filter: QuickFilter) => void;
  totalCount: number;
  filteredCount: number;

  // фильтр по типу документа
  availableDocTypes: string[];
  selectedDocType: string | null;
  onDocTypeChange: (value: string | null) => void;

  // новый: фильтр "есть структурированные данные"
  hasStructuredDataOnly: boolean;
  onHasStructuredDataChange: (value: boolean) => void;
}

const QUICK_FILTERS: { key: QuickFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "all", label: "Все" },
  { key: "critical", label: "Критические" },
  { key: "high", label: "Высокие" },
  { key: "unclear", label: "Неясные" },
  { key: "no_qc", label: "Без QC" },
  { key: "important", label: "Важные без QC" },
  { key: "conditional", label: "Conditional" },
  { key: "low_confidence", label: "Low confidence" },
];

/**
 * Отдельный компонент панели фильтров:
 * - ничего не знает о данных;
 * - полностью управляется через пропсы.
 */
const GridFilters: React.FC<GridFiltersProps> = ({
  searchText,
  onSearchChange,
  activeFilter,
  onFilterChange,
  totalCount,
  filteredCount,
  availableDocTypes,
  selectedDocType,
  onDocTypeChange,
  hasStructuredDataOnly,
  onHasStructuredDataChange,
}) => {
  const handleDocTypeChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    const v = e.target.value;
    onDocTypeChange(v === "" ? null : v);
  };

  const handleHasStructuredChange = (
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
  ) => {
    onHasStructuredDataChange(e.target.checked);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-center justify-between">
      {/* Поиск + счетчик */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={searchText}
          onChange={(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) =>
            onSearchChange(e.target.value)
          }
          placeholder="Поиск по документу или проверке…"
          className="text-xs border rounded px-2 py-1 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-400"
        />
        <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
          Всего: {totalCount}, после фильтра: {filteredCount}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Тип документа + чекбокс структурированных данных */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <span className="text-gray-500">Тип документа:</span>
          <select
            className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs"
            value={selectedDocType ?? ""}
            onChange={handleDocTypeChange}
          >
            <option value="">Все</option>
            {availableDocTypes.map((dt) => (
              <option key={dt} value={dt}>
                {dt}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            className="h-3 w-3"
            checked={hasStructuredDataOnly}
            onChange={handleHasStructuredChange}
          />
          <span className="text-gray-500">
            Только со структурированными данными
          </span>
        </label>
      </div>

      {/* Quick filters */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-xs">
        {QUICK_FILTERS.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.key}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onFilterChange(f.key)}
            className={
              activeFilter === f.key
                ? "px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white"
                : "px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"
            }
          >
            {f.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default GridFilters;

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { demoContent } from "../../../content/demoContent";

const DemoFormSection: React.FC = () => {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [company, setCompany] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [useCase, setUseCase] = useState("");

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    const subject = encodeURIComponent("Запрос демо LegalMAS");
    const body = encodeURIComponent(
      `Имя: ${name}\nКомпания: ${company}\nEmail: ${email}\n\nСценарий:\n${useCase}`
    );

    window.location.href =
      `mailto:vladimir.gorelov.mail@gmail.com?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
  };

  return (
    <section className="mt-16 grid lg:grid-cols-[1fr_0.8fr] gap-8 items-start">
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] p-8 lg:p-10">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{demoContent.form.title}</h2>
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-slate-400 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
          {demoContent.form.subtitle}
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-8 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
              {demoContent.form.fields.name}
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={demoContent.form.placeholders.name}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-white/30"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
              {demoContent.form.fields.company}
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={company}
              onChange={(e) => setCompany(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={demoContent.form.placeholders.company}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-white/30"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
              {demoContent.form.fields.email}
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={demoContent.form.placeholders.email}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-white/30"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
              {demoContent.form.fields.useCase}
            </label>
            <textarea
              required
              rows={6}
              value={useCase}
              onChange={(e) => setUseCase(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={demoContent.form.placeholders.useCase}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-white/30 resize-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{demoContent.form.note}</div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-950 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
          >
            {demoContent.form.submit}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <aside className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] p-8">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold">{demoContent.aside.title}</h3>
        <ul className="mt-6 space-y-4 text-sm text-slate-400">
          {demoContent.aside.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item}>- {item}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <div className="mt-8 text-sm text-slate-500">{demoContent.aside.time}</div>
      </aside>
    </section>
  );
};

export default DemoFormSection;

              {capabilities.subtitle}
            </p>
            <Link
              to="/capabilities"
              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-stone-900 transition-colors hover:text-stone-600"
            >
              Открыть все возможности
              <ArrowUpRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-12 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-12">

              {featuredPrimary.title}
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-5 max-w-2xl text-base leading-8 text-stone-700">
              {featuredPrimary.description}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-[32px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/70 p-7 lg:col-span-5 lg:p-9">
            <FeaturedSecondaryIcon className="h-6 w-6 text-stone-700" />
            <h3 className="mt-10 max-w-sm font-serif text-3xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950">
              {featuredSecondary.title}
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-5 text-sm leading-7 text-stone-700 lg:text-base">
              {featuredSecondary.description}
            </p>
          </div>

          {capabilities.support.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;

            return (
              <div
                key={item.title}
                className="rounded-[28px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/60 p-6 lg:col-span-4"
              >
                <Icon className="h-5 w-5 text-stone-700" />
                <h3 className="mt-8 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-stone-950">
                  {item.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-7 text-stone-700">{item.description}</p>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </MarketingContainer>
    </MarketingSection>
  );
};

export default CapabilitiesBentoSection;

import React from "react";
import { ArrowRight, Mail, Shield, X } from "lucide-react";
import { landingContent } from "../../../content/landingContent";

interface ContactModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  email: string;
  message: string;
  onEmailChange: (value: string) => void;
  onMessageChange: (value: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => void;
}

const ContactModal: React.FC<ContactModalProps> = ({
  open,
  email,
  message,
  onEmailChange,
  onMessageChange,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}) => {
  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-[rgba(32,27,22,0.48)] px-4 backdrop-blur-md"
      onClick={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}
    >
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-6xl overflow-hidden rounded-[36px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-[#f7f3ea] shadow-[0_32px_120px_rgba(28,25,23,0.18)]">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Закрыть"
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/80 text-stone-700 transition-colors hover:bg-white hover:text-stone-950"
        >
          <X className="h-5 w-5" />
        </button>

            <h3 className="mt-6 max-w-md font-serif text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950">

            <p className="mt-4 max-w-md text-sm leading-7 text-stone-700 lg:text-base">

import React from "react";
import { landingContent } from "../../../content/landingContent";
import MarketingContainer from "../primitives/MarketingContainer";
import MarketingSection from "../primitives/MarketingSection";

const PremiumHeroSection: React.FC = () => {
  const { hero } = landingContent;

  return (
    <MarketingSection className="overflow-hidden bg-[#f7f3ea] pt-8 sm:pt-10 lg:pt-14">
      <MarketingContainer size="hero">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-12 lg:items-end lg:gap-10">
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 xl:col-span-8">
            {hero.eyebrow ? (
              <div className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/70 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-stone-600">
                {hero.eyebrow}
              </div>
            ) : null}

            <h1
              className={`max-w-5xl font-serif text-5xl leading-[0.95] tracking-[-0.045em] text-stone-950 sm:text-6xl lg:text-[5.4rem] xl:text-[6.2rem] ${
                hero.eyebrow ? "mt-8" : "mt-0"
              }`}
            >
              {hero.title}
            </h1>

            <div className="mt-8 max-w-2xl space-y-3 text-base leading-8 text-stone-700 lg:text-[1.14rem]">
              {hero.descriptionLines.map((line) => (
                <p key={line}>{line}</p>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-5 xl:col-span-4">
            <div className="rounded-[32px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-[#ece5d8] p-6 shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(28,25,23,0.08)] lg:p-7">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-stone-500">
                Review posture
              </div>

              <h2 className="mt-4 max-w-sm font-serif text-3xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950">
                {hero.panelTitle}
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
                {hero.panelItems.map((item, index) => (
                  <div
                    key={item}
                    className="rounded-[22px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/80 px-4 py-4"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-stone-950 text-xs font-semibold text-[#f7f3ea]">
                        0{index + 1}
                      </div>

                    </div>

                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

      </MarketingContainer>
    </MarketingSection>
  );
};

import { landingContent } from "../../../content/landingContent";
import MarketingContainer from "../primitives/MarketingContainer";
import MarketingSection from "../primitives/MarketingSection";

  return (
    <MarketingSection id="use-cases" className="bg-[#f7f3ea] scroll-mt-24">
      <MarketingContainer size="section">
        <div className="grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="lg:col-span-4">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-stone-500">
              Use cases
            </div>
            <h2 className="mt-4 max-w-md font-serif text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950 lg:text-5xl">
              {useCases.title}
            </h2>
            <Link
              to="/solutions"
              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-stone-900 transition-colors hover:text-stone-600"
            >
              Открыть все сценарии
              <ArrowUpRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Link>

            <div className="mt-8 rounded-[28px] border border-stone-900/10 bg-white/70 p-6 lg:p-7">
              <SearchCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-stone-700" />
              <h3 className="mt-10 max-w-sm font-serif text-3xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950">
                Проверка активов \ партнеров
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-4 max-w-lg text-sm leading-7 text-stone-700 lg:text-base">
                Проведение дополнительной проверки активов \ контрагентов из анализируемых
                документов по выбранным критериям через сторонние сервисы и агрегирование
                результатов их проверки в основной отчет.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:col-span-8">
            {useCases.items.map((item, index) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              const tones = ["bg-white/70", "bg-[#efe9dd]", "bg-[#f2ece2]", "bg-white/75"];

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.title}
                  className={`rounded-[28px] border border-stone-900/10 p-6 lg:p-7 ${tones[index % tones.length]}`}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-5 w-5 text-stone-700" />
                  <h3 className="mt-10 max-w-sm font-serif text-3xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.03em] text-stone-950">
                    {item.title}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-4 max-w-lg text-sm leading-7 text-stone-700 lg:text-base">
                    {item.description}
                  </p>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </MarketingContainer>
    </MarketingSection>
  );
};

export default UseCasesSection;

                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </MarketingContainer>
    </MarketingSection>
  );
};

export const marketingTheme = {
  container: {
    hero: "mx-auto w-full max-w-[1480px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10 xl:px-12",
    section: "mx-auto w-full max-w-[1360px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 xl:px-10",
    prose: "mx-auto w-full max-w-[880px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8",
  },

  section: {
    base: "py-20 lg:py-28",
    compact: "py-14 lg:py-18",
    relaxed: "py-24 lg:py-32",
  },

  surface: {
    subtle: "bg-white/[0.025] border border-white/10",
    soft: "bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10",
    strong: "bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/10",
    elevated: "bg-white/[0.05] border border-white/10 shadow-2xl shadow-black/30",
  },

  radius: {
    sm: "rounded-xl",
    md: "rounded-2xl",
    lg: "rounded-[28px]",
    xl: "rounded-[32px]",
    hero: "rounded-[36px]",
    pill: "rounded-full",

  text: {
    eyebrow: "text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-slate-500",

    cardTitle: "text-xl font-semibold text-white",
    cardTitleLg: "text-2xl font-semibold text-white lg:text-3xl",
  },

  grid: {
    two: "grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2",

    four: "grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4",

    label: "mb-2 block text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-500",

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { marketingTheme } from "../marketingTheme";

interface MarketingBadgeProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  tone?: "default" | "success";
}

const MarketingBadge: React.FC<MarketingBadgeProps> = ({
  children,
  className,
  tone = "default",
}) => {
  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(
        tone === "success" ? marketingTheme.badge.success : marketingTheme.badge.base,
        className
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MarketingBadge;

>>> FILE_START | index=73 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/marketing/primitives/MarketingButton.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { marketingTheme } from "../marketingTheme";

interface MarketingButtonProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  to?: string;
  onClick?: () => void;
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "tertiary";
  className?: string;
}

const MarketingButton: React.FC<MarketingButtonProps> = ({
  children,
  to,
  onClick,
  variant = "primary",
  className,
}) => {
  const classes = clsx(marketingTheme.button[variant], className);

  if (to) {
    return (
      <Link to={to} className={classes}>
        {children}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className={classes}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

export default MarketingButton;

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { marketingTheme } from "../marketingTheme";

interface MarketingContainerProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  size?: "hero" | "section" | "prose";
  className?: string;
}

const MarketingContainer: React.FC<MarketingContainerProps> = ({
  children,
  size = "section",
  className,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className={clsx(marketingTheme.container[size], className)}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MarketingContainer;

import React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { marketingTheme } from "../marketingTheme";

interface MarketingSectionProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  id?: string;
  density?: "base" | "compact" | "relaxed";
}

const densityMap = {
  base: marketingTheme.section.base,
  compact: marketingTheme.section.compact,
  relaxed: marketingTheme.section.relaxed,
};

const MarketingSection: React.FC<MarketingSectionProps> = ({
  children,
  className,
  id,
  density = "base",
}) => {
  return (
    <section id={id} className={clsx(densityMap[density], className)}>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
};

export default MarketingSection;

>>> FILE_START | index=90 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/qc/BulkQCBar.tsx
================================================================================
// =============================================================
// src/components/qc/BulkQCBar.tsx
// Панель массового QC для выбранных результатов.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";
import type { CheckResultValue, SeverityLevel } from "../../types";

interface BulkQCBarProps {
  selectedCount: number;
  onConfirm: (comment: string) => void;
  onReject: (comment: string) => void;
  onOverride?: (params: {
    newSeverity: SeverityLevel;
    newResult?: CheckResultValue;
    comment: string;
  }) => void;
}

// Варианты severity для override
const SEVERITY_OPTIONS: { value: SeverityLevel; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "critical", label: "Critical" },
  { value: "high",     label: "High" },
  { value: "medium",   label: "Medium" },
  { value: "pass",     label: "Pass" },
  { value: "unclear",  label: "Unclear" },
];

// Варианты result для override (+ «не менять»)
const RESULT_OPTIONS: { value: CheckResultValue | ""; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "",        label: "Не менять result" },
  { value: "pass",    label: "Pass" },
  { value: "fail",    label: "Fail" },
  { value: "unclear", label: "Unclear" },
];

export const BulkQCBar: React.FC<BulkQCBarProps> = ({
  selectedCount,
  onConfirm,
  onReject,
  onOverride,
}) => {
  const [comment, setComment] = React.useState("");
  const [showOverride, setShowOverride] = React.useState(false);
  const [newSeverity, setNewSeverity] = React.useState<SeverityLevel>("medium");
  const [newResult, setNewResult] = React.useState<CheckResultValue | "">("");

  const disabled = selectedCount === 0;

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (disabled) return;
    onConfirm(comment.trim());
    setComment("");
  };

  const handleReject = () => {
    if (disabled) return;
    onReject(comment.trim());
    setComment("");
  };

  const handleOverride = () => {
    if (disabled || !onOverride) return;
    onOverride({
      newSeverity,
      newResult: newResult !== "" ? newResult : undefined,
      comment: comment.trim(),
    });
    setComment("");
    setShowOverride(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-30 border-t bg-white/95 backdrop-blur px-4 py-2 text-xs space-y-2">
      {/* Основная строка */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="text-gray-700">
          Выбрано результатов:{" "}
          <span className="font-semibold">{selectedCount}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={comment}
            onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Комментарий QC (опционально)…"
            className="text-xs border rounded px-2 py-1 w-64 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-400"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-green-700 border-green-300 hover:bg-green-50 disabled:opacity-50"
            onClick={handleConfirm}
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            Подтвердить
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-red-700 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
            onClick={handleReject}
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            Отклонить
          </button>
          {onOverride && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs disabled:opacity-50 ${
                showOverride
                  ? "bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 border-orange-300"
                  : "text-gray-700 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50"
              }`}
              onClick={() => setShowOverride((v) => !v)}
              disabled={disabled}
            >
              Override…
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Секция Override */}
      {showOverride && onOverride && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t pt-2">
          <span className="text-gray-500">Override:</span>

          {/* Severity */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <label className="text-gray-500">Severity:</label>
            <select
              className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs"
              value={newSeverity}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setNewSeverity(e.target.value as SeverityLevel)
              }
            >
              {SEVERITY_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Result */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <label className="text-gray-500">Result:</label>
            <select
              className="border rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs"
              value={newResult}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setNewResult(e.target.value as CheckResultValue | "")
              }
            >
              {RESULT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-3 py-1 rounded bg-orange-600 text-white text-xs hover:bg-orange-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            onClick={handleOverride}
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            Применить override
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-3 py-1 rounded border text-xs text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50"
            onClick={() => setShowOverride(false)}
          >
            Отмена
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default BulkQCBar;

================================================================================
<<< FILE_END | index=90 | path=src/components/qc/BulkQCBar.tsx
================================================================================


================================================================================
>>> FILE_START | index=91 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/qc/QCCellRenderer.tsx
================================================================================
// =============================================================
// src/components/qc/QCCellRenderer.tsx
// Отдельная колонка QC-статуса + быстрые действия.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";
import type { CheckResult, QCStatus } from "../../types";

interface QCCellRendererProps {
  value: QCStatus | undefined;
  data: CheckResult;
  onQcChange?: (
    result: CheckResult,
    newStatus: "approved" | "rejected",
  ) => void;
}

export const QCCellRenderer: React.FC<QCCellRendererProps> = ({
  value,
  data,
  onQcChange,
}) => {
  const status: QCStatus | undefined = value;

  const handleApprove = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onQcChange?.(data, "approved");
  };

  const handleReject = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onQcChange?.(data, "rejected");
  };

  const label: QCStatus = status ?? "pending";

  let color = "text-gray-600";
  if (label === "approved") color = "text-green-600";
  if (label === "rejected") color = "text-red-600";
  if (label === "needs_review") color = "text-orange-600";

  const isPending = label === "pending";

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className={`text-[11px] ${color}`}>{label}</span>
      {onQcChange && (
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-[10px] px-1 py-0.5 border rounded hover:bg-green-50 disabled:opacity-40"
            onClick={handleApprove}
            title="Подтвердить (QC approve)"
            disabled={!isPending}
          >
            ✅
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-[10px] px-1 py-0.5 border rounded hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-40"
            onClick={handleReject}
            title="Отклонить (QC reject)"
            disabled={!isPending}
          >
            ❌
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default QCCellRenderer;

// =============================================================
// src/components/qc/QualityFlagRenderer.tsx
// Компактный рендерер флагов качества.
// =============================================================

import React from "react";
import type { CheckResult } from "../../types";

interface QualityFlagRendererProps {
  result: CheckResult;
}

export const QualityFlagRenderer: React.FC<QualityFlagRendererProps> = ({
  result,
}) => {
  // quality_flags приходит как словарь вида { [ключ]: boolean }
  const flags: Record<string, boolean> = result.quality_flags ?? {};

  const hasStructureDegraded = Boolean(flags.structure_degraded);
  const hasLlmFallback = Boolean(flags.llm_fallback_used);

  const otherFlagsExist =
    Object.keys(flags).filter(
      (k) => k !== "structure_degraded" && k !== "llm_fallback_used",
    ).length > 0;

  if (!hasStructureDegraded && !hasLlmFallback && !otherFlagsExist) {
    return <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-300">—</span>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
      {hasStructureDegraded && (
        <span
          className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orange-500"
          title="Нарушена структура документа"
        />
      )}
      {hasLlmFallback && (
        <span
          className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-purple-500"
          title="Использован LLM fallback"
        />
      )}
      {otherFlagsExist && (
        <span
          className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-gray-500"
          title="Другие флаги качества"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default QualityFlagRenderer;

>>> FILE_START | index=94 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/requests/RequestSidePanel.tsx
================================================================================
// =============================================================
// src/components/requests/RequestSidePanel.tsx
// Side panel деталей DD-запроса для юриста.
// - Приоритет (селектор)
// - Статус (селектор)
// - Текст запроса (редактируемый)
// - Адресат
// - Источник (badge + пояснение для yaml_template)
// - Связанные документы
// - Проверка (check_id)
// - Извлечённые данные (structured_values)
// - Действия: копировать текст, сохранить, удалить
// =============================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { DDRequest } from "../../types";
import { DD_REQUEST_SOURCE_DISPLAY } from "../../constants";
import { X, Clipboard, Check, Trash2, Pencil, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";

interface Props {
  request: DDRequest | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (reqId: string, data: Partial<DDRequest>) => Promise<void>;
  onDelete: (reqId: string) => Promise<void>;
  isSaving: boolean;
  canManageRequests: boolean;
}

export const RequestSidePanel: React.FC<Props> = ({
  request,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  onDelete,
  isSaving,
  canManageRequests,
}) => {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [priority, setPriority] =
    useState<DDRequest["priority"]>("medium");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<DDRequest["status"]>("open");
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  // Синхронизация при выборе нового запроса.
  // Привязываемся только к идентификатору, чтобы сбрасывать форму
  // когда пользователь выбрал другой request.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!request) {
      return;
    }
    setText(request.text);
    setPriority(request.priority);
    setStatus(request.status);
    setEditing(false);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [request?.id]);

  // Закрытие по Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
  }, [onClose]);

  if (!request) return null;

  // Копирование текста
  const handleCopy = async () => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  // Сохранение изменений
  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!canManageRequests) return;
    await onSave(request.id, { text, priority, status });
    setEditing(false);
  };

  // Удаление запроса
  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!canManageRequests) return;
    if (!confirm("Удалить запрос? Это действие нельзя отменить.")) return;
    await onDelete(request.id);
    onClose();
  };

  // Приоритеты — фиксированный список (из типа)
  const PRIORITY_OPTIONS: {
    value: DDRequest["priority"];
    label: string;
  }[] = [
    { value: "critical", label: "Критичный" },
    { value: "high", label: "Высокий" },
    { value: "medium", label: "Средний" },
  ];

  const STATUS_OPTIONS: {
    value: DDRequest["status"];
    label: string;
  }[] = [
    { value: "open", label: "Открыт" },
    { value: "in_progress", label: "В работе" },
    { value: "resolved", label: "Закрыт" },
  ];

  const sourceCfg = request.source
    ? DD_REQUEST_SOURCE_DISPLAY[request.source]
    : undefined;

  const documentCount = Array.isArray(request.document_refs)
    ? request.document_refs.length
    : 0;

  return (
    <>
      {/* Затемнение */}
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/20 z-40"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      {/* Панель */}
      <div className="fixed right-0 top-0 h-full w-[420px] bg-white shadow-2xl z-50 border-l flex flex-col animate-in slide-in-from-right">
        {/* Шапка */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
            Запрос #{request.id.slice(-6)}
          </h3>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded transition"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Тело */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-5">
          {/* Приоритет */}
          <Field label="Приоритет">
            <select
              value={priority}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setPriority(e.target.value as DDRequest["priority"])
              }
              disabled={!editing || !canManageRequests}
              className="w-full p-2 border rounded text-sm disabled:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            >
              {PRIORITY_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Field>

          {/* Статус */}
          <Field label="Статус">
            <select
              value={status}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setStatus(e.target.value as DDRequest["status"])
              }
              disabled={!editing || !canManageRequests}
              className="w-full p-2 border rounded text-sm disabled:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            >
              {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
                  {opt.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Field>

          {/* Адресат */}
          {request.addressee && (
            <Field label="Адресат">
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 font-medium">
                {request.addressee}
              </p>
            </Field>
          )}

          {/* Источник */}
          {request.source && (
            <Field label="Источник">
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-medium ${
                  sourceCfg?.color || "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
                }`}
              >
                {sourceCfg?.icon && (
                  <span aria-hidden="true">{sourceCfg.icon}</span>
                )}
                <span>{sourceCfg?.label || request.source}</span>
              </span>
              {request.source === "yaml_template" && (
                <p className="text-xs text-purple-600 mt-1">
                  Текст и адресат сгенерированы автоматически из
                  YAML-инструкции типа документа.
                </p>
              )}
            </Field>
          )}

          {/* Текст запроса */}
          <Field label="Текст запроса">
            <textarea
              value={text}
              onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
              rows={8}
              disabled={!editing || !canManageRequests}
              className="w-full p-2 border rounded text-sm resize-y disabled:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            />
          </Field>

          {/* Документы */}
          <Field label={`Документы (${documentCount})`}>
            <div className="space-y-1 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
              {request.document_refs.slice(0, 10).map((docId, i) => (
                <div
                  key={docId ?? i}
                  className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-700 p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded"
                >
                  <span>📄</span>
                  <span className="truncate">{docId}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {documentCount > 10 && (
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 py-1 text-center">
                  … и ещё {documentCount - 10}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </Field>

          {/* Проверка */}
          <Field label="Проверка">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700">
              {request.check_id ?? "—"}
            </p>
            {request.source === "yaml_template" && (
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                Связано с YAML‑проверкой (check_id).
              </p>
            )}
          </Field>

          {/* Извлечённые данные */}
          {request.structured_values &&
            request.structured_values.length > 0 && (
              <Field
                label={`Извлечённые данные (${request.structured_values.length})`}
              >
                <div className="space-y-1 bg-gray-50 rounded p-2 max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
                  {request.structured_values.map((sv, i) => {
                    const [key] = Object.keys(sv);
                    const [val] = Object.values(sv);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={i}
                        className="flex justify-between text-xs py-0.5 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0"
                      >
                        <span className="text-gray-400 capitalize">
                          {String(key || "").replace(/_/g, " ")}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-gray-700 font-medium text-right">
                          {val != null ? String(val) : "—"}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </Field>
            )}
        </div>

        {/* Подвал */}
        <div className="border-t p-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
          {/* Копировать — доступно всем */}
          <button
            onClick={handleCopy}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 w-full px-4 py-2 border rounded text-sm hover:bg-gray-50 transition"
          >
            {copied ? (
              <>
                <Check size={14} className="text-green-600" />
                <span className="text-green-600">Скопировано!</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Clipboard size={14} />
                Копировать текст
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Редактирование / сохранение */}
          {editing ? (
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={handleSave}
                disabled={isSaving || !canManageRequests}
                className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded text-sm font-medium hover:bg-green-700 transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isSaving ? (
                  <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={14} />
                ) : (
                  <Check size={14} />
                )}
                Сохранить
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  // Явный откат к значениям текущего запроса
                  setText(request.text);
                  setPriority(request.priority);
                  setStatus(request.status);
                  setEditing(false);
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 border rounded text-sm text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 transition"
              >
                Отмена
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => canManageRequests && setEditing(true)}
                disabled={!canManageRequests}
                className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded text-sm font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Pencil size={14} />
                Редактировать
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleDelete}
                disabled={!canManageRequests}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 px-4 py-2 border border-red-200 text-red-600 rounded text-sm hover:bg-red-50 transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

function Field({
  label,
  children,
}: {
  label: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
        {label}
      </label>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export default RequestSidePanel;

>>> FILE_START | index=95 | type=React TSX | path=src/components/requests/RequestsTable.tsx
================================================================================
// =============================================================
// src/components/requests/RequestsTable.tsx
// Таблица DD‑запросов для юриста.
// Колонки: Приоритет, Текст, Документы (count), Статус, Источник.
// Поиск, сортировка, кнопки экспорта.
// =============================================================

import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { DDRequest } from "../../types";
import {
  DD_REQUEST_STATUS_DISPLAY,
  DD_REQUEST_SOURCE_DISPLAY,
} from "../../constants";
import { Search, Plus, Download, FileText } from "lucide-react";

interface Props {
  requests: DDRequest[];
  onRowClick: (request: DDRequest) => void;
  onCreateClick: () => void;
  onExportWord: () => Promise<void>;
  onExportExcel: () => Promise<void>;
  canManageRequests: boolean; 

}

type SortKey = "priority" | "status" | "created";

const PRIORITY_ORDER: Record<string, number> = {
  critical: 0,
  high: 1,
  medium: 2,
};

export const RequestsTable: React.FC<Props> = ({
  requests,
  onRowClick,
  onCreateClick,
  onExportWord,
  onExportExcel,
  canManageRequests,
}) => {
  const [searchText, setSearchText] = useState("");
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<SortKey>("priority");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = [...requests];

    if (searchText.trim()) {
      const lower = searchText.toLowerCase();
      list = list.filter((r) => {
        const text = r.text.toLowerCase();
        const checkName = r.check_id?.toLowerCase() ?? "";
        return text.includes(lower) || checkName.includes(lower);
      });
    }

    list.sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortBy === "priority") {
        return (
          (PRIORITY_ORDER[a.priority] ?? 9) -
          (PRIORITY_ORDER[b.priority] ?? 9)
        );
      }
      if (sortBy === "status") {
        return a.status.localeCompare(b.status);
      }
      // created (newest first)
      return (
        new Date(b.created_at).getTime() -
        new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
      );
    });

    return list;
  }, [requests, searchText, sortBy]);

return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Шапка */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900">
          Запросы ({requests.length})
        </h2>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {canManageRequests && (
            <button
              onClick={onCreateClick}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded text-sm font-medium hover:bg-blue-700 transition"
            >
              <Plus size={14} />
              Новый запрос
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={onExportWord}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 rounded text-sm hover:bg-gray-200 transition"
          >
            <FileText size={14} />
            Word
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onExportExcel}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 rounded text-sm hover:bg-gray-200 transition"
          >
            <Download size={14} />
            Excel
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Поиск */}
      <div className="relative">
        <Search
          className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"
          size={16}
        />
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Поиск по тексту запроса или ID проверки…"
          value={searchText}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchText(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 border rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Таблица */}
      <div className="border rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
        <table className="w-full">
          <thead>
            <tr className="bg-gray-50">
              {(
                [
                  ["priority", "Приоритет", "w-28"],
                  ["text", "Текст запроса", ""],
                  ["docs", "Документы", "w-24"],
                  ["status", "Статус", "w-28"],
                  ["source", "Источник", "w-32"],
                ] as const
              ).map(([key, label, width]) => (
                <th
                  key={key}
                  className={`px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider ${width}`}
                >
                  {key === "priority" || key === "status" ? (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSortBy(key as SortKey)}
                      className={`hover:text-gray-700 ${
                        sortBy === key ? "text-blue-600 underline" : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      {label}
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    label
                  )}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
            {filtered.map((req) => {
              const statusDisplay =
                DD_REQUEST_STATUS_DISPLAY[req.status] ?? req.status;
              const [statusIcon, statusLabel] =
                statusDisplay.includes(" ")
                  ? statusDisplay.split(" ", 2)
                  : ["", statusDisplay];

              const sourceCfg = req.source
                ? DD_REQUEST_SOURCE_DISPLAY[req.source]
                : undefined;

              const documentCount = Array.isArray(req.document_refs)
                ? req.document_refs.length
                : 0;

              return (
                <tr
                  key={req.id}
                  className="hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition"
                  onClick={() => onRowClick(req)}
                >
                  {/* Приоритет */}
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span
                      className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold ${
                        req.priority === "critical"
                          ? "bg-red-100 text-red-800"
                          : req.priority === "high"
                          ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-800"
                          : "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {req.priority === "critical"
                        ? "Критичный"
                        : req.priority === "high"
                        ? "Высокий"
                        : "Средний"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  {/* Текст */}
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-900 line-clamp-2">
                      {req.text}
                    </p>
                    {req.check_id && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                        Проверка: {req.check_id}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </td>

                  {/* Документы */}
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-700">
                    {documentCount} док.
                  </td>

                  {/* Статус */}
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
                      {statusIcon && (
                        <span aria-hidden="true">{statusIcon}</span>
                      )}
                      <span>{statusLabel}</span>
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  {/* Источник */}
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    {sourceCfg ? (
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${sourceCfg.color}`}
                      >
                        {sourceCfg.icon && (
                          <span aria-hidden="true">
                            {sourceCfg.icon}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        <span>{sourceCfg.label}</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-gray-300 text-xs">—</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>

        {filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-16 text-gray-400">
            <p className="text-lg mb-1">Запросы не найдены</p>
            <p className="text-sm">
              {requests.length === 0
                ? "Запросы появятся после анализа с найденными проблемами"
                : "Попробуйте изменить поиск"}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default RequestsTable;
